## anomaly_pulsating_object_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_pulsating_object_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[this.GetName]§!的初步扫描发现了一个特殊的空中物体，漂浮在对流层中，随着位置的改变而涨缩。为探明其性质，需要进行进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

Initial scans of §Y[this.GetName]§! have discovered a peculiar airborne object floating through the troposphere, shrinking and swelling in size as it changes positions. Further investigation is necessary in order to classify its nature.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:16`

## extreme_frontiers.100.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.100.title&lang=en)

### 中文

古代气象气球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

Ancient Weather Balloon

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:17`

## extreme_frontiers.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!辨认出了在§Y[from.GetName]§!的神秘飞行物是一颗古老的气象气球。这颗行星上极端的大气条件已经将其上任何可能用来辨识其起源的信息都抹除殆尽了。\n\n放射性定年表明它已经在[from.GetName]的大气层中至少飘了一个世纪，它在行星特有的压力变化中随波逐流，大小也随之涨缩。\n\n不可思议的是，它的太阳能电池板仍然在运行，上面的传感器阵列现在携带着一个世纪的宝贵大气数据。这激起了我们[Root.Owner.GetResearchers]的极大兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

§Y[Leader.GetName]§! has identified the mysterious airborne object on §Y[from.GetName]§! as an archaic weather balloon. Any markings that could have identified its origin have long since been stripped away by the planet's extreme atmospheric conditions.\n\nRadiometric dating suggests it has been present in [from.GetName]'s atmosphere for over a century, fluctuating in size as it travels listlessly through the planet's idiosyncratic pressure variations.\n\nMiraculously, the solar energy panels are still functioning and its sensor array now carries a century's worth of valuable atmospheric data. It will be of great interest to our [Root.Owner.GetResearchers].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:18`

## MOLTEN_METAL_OCEAN_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMOLTEN_METAL_OCEAN_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

极端熔岩流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

Extreme Molten Currents

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:21`

## anomaly_molten_metal_ocean_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_molten_metal_ocean_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!地表的初步调查发现这里经常发生一种含有高浓度重金属的超热熔岩海啸。必须通过进一步分析才能完全理解这种现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

Initial surveys of §Y[from.GetName]'s§! surface have detected frequent tsunamis of superheated lava containing high concentrations of heavy metals. Further analysis will be necessary to fully understand this phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:22`

## extreme_frontiers.110.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.110.title&lang=en)

### 中文

金属海啸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

Metallic Tsunamis

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:23`

## extreme_frontiers.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!确认§Y[from.GetName]§!上的熔岩海洋含有元素周期表中几乎所有已知稀有金属的极纯形式。\n\n这种高浓度的液态金属汇聚产生狂暴的电磁场，导致熔融金属海啸的频发。\n\n我们利用预测模型确定了海啸之间存在的短暂但有规律的间歇，可以利用来收获这些独特的丰富矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:20`

### English

§Y[Leader.GetName]§! has confirmed that the lava oceans on §Y[from.GetName]§! contain exceptionally pure forms of almost every known rare metalloid in our elemental catalog.\n\nThis concentrated collection of liquid metals gives rise to highly volatile electromagnetic fields, causing the frequent formation of molten metal tsunamis.\n\nUsing predictive modeling, we have identified brief but regular lulls in wave intensity which can be used to harvest these uniquely rich deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:24`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGREAT_BLUE_SPOTS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

## great_blue_spots_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreat_blue_spots_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的地表分布着泾渭分明的两种色彩以极快的速度串联移动着，在太空中亦清晰可见。这些蓝色的结构相当有趣，值得进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

The surface of §Y[from.GetName]§! features two distinctive discolorations, large enough to be visible from space and moving in tandem at great speeds. These blue-colored formations are interesting enough to warrant further study.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:28`

## extreme_frontiers.120.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.120.title&lang=en)

### 中文

共生超级风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:24`

### English

Symbiotic Super Storms

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:29`

## extreme_frontiers.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上的大蓝点其实是一对双生超级风暴；一个气旋和一个反气旋互相支持形成了一种完美的平衡。\n\n这对风暴规模巨大，风速通常都突破音障，持续发出强烈音爆。风暴中含有大量的钴金属尘，使其显出了独特的蓝色。\n\n§Y[Leader.GetName]§!估计这对风暴已经在这呼啸了超过一个世纪，其在上层大气所积累的矿物应该用开采站就很容易采集起来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

The large blue spots on §Y[from.GetName]§! are a pair of symbiotic super storms; a cyclone and anticyclone that sustain each other in a state of perfect equilibrium.\n\nThe storms are massive in scale with wind speeds regularly breaking the sound barrier, creating powerful sonic booms. Vast volumes of cobalt dust are present within, giving the spots their distinctive color.\n\n§Y[Leader.GetName]§! estimates they have been raging continuously here for over a century. The minerals they have accumulated in the upper atmosphere should be easily recoverable with a mining station.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:30`

## frozen_inferno_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrozen_inferno_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的初步扫描发现在这颗冰冻的天体上存在极不正常的热量峰值。这种温度异常亟待进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

Initial scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! are showing extremely erratic heat spikes on this otherwise frozen celestial body. These anomalous temperatures elicit further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:34`

## extreme_frontiers.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!完成了对§Y[from.GetName]§!这颗冰封星球上异常热点的调查。\n\n在星球表面无尽的冻土带下埋藏着高度活泼的火山沉积物。这些物质起初处于惰性状态深埋冰下，但随着大陆冰盖的漂移和破裂被周期性地向上输送。\n\n这些超易燃气体在上升到地表时很容易在[from.GetName]的富氧大气中猝燃，频繁地在行星表面发生闪爆。\n\n这些独特的易燃物质可以使用特殊设备加以提取并在别处排上用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:30`

### English

§Y[Leader.GetName]§! concluded their investigation of the erratic heat spikes on the frozen world of §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nBuried beneath the unending tundra of the planet's surface are highly reactive pyrophoric deposits. Initially kept inert deep beneath the ice, these substances are periodically sifted upwards as the continental ice sheets drift and break apart.\n\nThawing as they rise to the surface, these ultra-flammable gases are prone to sudden ignition in [from.GetName]'s oxygen-rich atmosphere, causing frequent flash fires across the surface of the planet.\n\nWith specialized equipment, these uniquely combustible substances can be extracted and put to use elsewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:36`

## extreme_frontiers.140.desc.hive_mind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.140.desc.hive_mind&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!是一种非凡的地衣物种的家园，它们将剧毒分子排入大气中，以保护自己免受捕食者的侵害。\n\n化石记录的研究得知[from.GetName]曾经有丰富多样的物种，但这种地衣以超出生物所能适应的速度梯度提高了大气中的砷和铊浓度，这些物种都被被逐一灭绝了。\n\n§Y[Leader.GetName]§!只发现了另一种存活的有机体，是一种大型类爬行动物，它们要靠特别高效的解毒腺体在这种环境中存活下来。\n\n对这种非凡器官的进一步研究可能会对大气解毒产生洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! is home to a remarkable species of lichen which expels ultra-toxic molecules into the atmosphere as a method to secure itself against predation.\n\nStudies of the fossil record suggest [from.GetName] once had a diverse range of fauna which the lichen has killed off over time by gradually raising levels of arsenic and thallium in the atmosphere faster than most could adapt.\n\n§Y[Leader.GetName]§! discovered only one other living organism, a species of large reptiloids, has been able to survive to such conditions thanks to a uniquely efficient detoxification gland.\n\nFurther study of this remarkable organ could yield insights into atmospheric detoxification.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:42`

## extreme_frontiers.140.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.140.desc.default&lang=en)

$extreme_frontiers.140.desc.hive_mind$初步试验还表明，有毒物质中的独特化合物还可以用于宇宙工业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.140.desc.hive_mind$ Initial experiments also indicate that the unique compounds inside the poisons could be repurposed for use in the cosmetic industry.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:43`

## DETOXIFICATION_GLAND_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADETOXIFICATION_GLAND_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种非凡的类爬行物种在§Y[from.GetName]§!的剧毒大气中欣欣向荣，其秘诀就是一种可以将极端毒素破坏成有用养分的独特内器官。对这种有机体的进一步研究值得一试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

A remarkable species of reptiloid thrives in the toxic atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§!, aided by a unique internal organ which breaks extreme toxins down into useful nutrients. Further study of this organism would be a worthy endeavor.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:47`

## extreme_frontiers.145.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.145.title&lang=en)

### 中文

排毒洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Detoxification Insight

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:48`

## extreme_frontiers.145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!上发现的类爬行类腺体进行了深入研究之后，§Y[Leader.GetName]§!发现它们对行星毒性独特抵抗力背后的生物学秘密。\n\n这种复杂的器官会分泌一种可以对毒性大气起到中和作用的化合物，避免这种类爬行动物的循环系统吸收毒素。这种极具适应力的腺体甚至可以中和掉我们实验室制造的最致命的毒素。\n\n这些非凡的发现将彻底改变我们大气排毒的方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

Through intensive study of the reptiloid glands on §Y[from.GetName]§!, §Y[Leader.GetName]§! has uncovered the biological secrets of their remarkable resistance to the planet's toxicity.\n\nThis complex organ produces compounds which act as neutralizing agents to the poisonous atmosphere, preventing toxins from being absorbed by the reptile's circulatory system. This highly adaptive gland is even able to nullify our most lethal lab-generated poisons.\n\nThese extraordinary findings will transform our approaches to atmospheric detoxification.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:49`

## glimpse_the_core_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aglimpse_the_core_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!外层气体的怪异活动打开了一些极深的开口，短暂地暴露出行星的固体核心。这一独特现象值得进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Strange movements in §Y[from.GetName]'s§! gassy outer layers are causing exceptionally deep vents to open up, exposing the planet's solid core in brief intervals. This remarkable phenomenon warrants further study. 

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:53`

## extreme_frontiers.150.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.150.title&lang=en)

### 中文

有机内核

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

Organic Core

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:54`

## extreme_frontiers.150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过§Y[from.GetName]§!上的暂时开口送入探测器，§Y[Leader.GetName]§!发现行星的内核是一种单细胞有机物种的家园，它们与此前见过的一切生物都不相似。\n\n这些强韧的细胞似乎完美地适应了在极端压力下的生活，它们季节性的繁殖活动会排出体积可观的氢气。[Leader.GetName]认为这就是行星之所以能维持氢气层的原因。\n\n消灭这些生物能使外层的消散，获得一颗可殖民的石质行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

By sending a probe deep into one of the temporary vents on §Y[from.GetName]§!, §Y[Leader.GetName]§! discovered that the planet's core is home to a species of unicellular organisms unlike anything we have seen before.\n\nThese robust cells seem perfectly adapted for survival under extreme pressures, with their seasonal reproduction releasing sizable quantities of hydrogen. [Leader.GetName] has concluded that this is how the planet's gassy hydrogen layers are being maintained.\n\nExterminating these organisms would cause the dissipation of these outer layers, leaving behind a colonizable rocky planet.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:55`

## extreme_frontiers.150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

消灭它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Exterminate them.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:56`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.150.b&lang=en)

这是个独特的观测机会。

### English

A unique observation opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:57`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APURIFY_CORE_PROJECT&lang=en)

净化核心

### English

Purify the Core

## PURIFY_CORE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APURIFY_CORE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的气态外层由一种生活在其核心上的单细胞生物独特的生化过程所维持。将这些生物消灭可以让气体消散，使其下的岩质行星得以宜居。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]'s§! gassy outer layers are maintained by the unique biochemistry of unicellular organisms which inhabit its core. Exterminating them would dissipate these gases, leaving us with a colonizable rocky planet.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:59`

## extreme_frontiers.155.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.155.title&lang=en)

### 中文

新世界

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

A New World

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:60`

## extreme_frontiers.155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!通过§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上的一个开口送入了一台武器化的探测器。一抵达核心，其上的有毒载荷就会释放，侵染并灭绝当地的单细胞生物。\n\n我们发现[from.From.GetName]的气态外层逐渐消散在虚空中，余下的是一颗小巧的岩质行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

§Y[Leader.GetName]§! sent a weaponized probe into one of the openings on §Y[from.From.GetName]'s§! surface. Once at the core, a toxic payload was released, infecting and exterminating its native single-celled organisms.\n\nWe observed as [from.From.GetName]'s gassy outer layers gradually dissipated into the void, leaving behind a small and rocky planet.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:61`

### English

An impressive feat.

## optical_phenomena_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoptical_phenomena_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的影像扫描显示行星表面有极其活跃的光学现象，尽管这些颜色图案的成因在轨道距离上尚未探明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Our imaging scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! are returning extremely vibrant optical phenomena across the planet's surface, though the cause of these color patterns remains unclear at an orbital distance.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:66`

## extreme_frontiers.160.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.160.title&lang=en)

### 中文

彩虹冰盖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Rainbow Ice Sheet

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:67`

## extreme_frontiers.160.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.160.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!地表的进一步调查发现了我们在轨调查时遇到的这些光学现象的成因。\n\n大气条件将星球的大部分永久冻土打磨出透明的光泽，揭示了隐藏其下的生长着多色晶体的深层网络。从远处观察，这些自然现象产生了如万花筒般的效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Closer inspection of §Y[from.GetName]§!'s surface discovered the cause of the optical phenomena encountered during our orbital surveys.\n\nAtmospheric conditions have polished large sections of the planet's permafrost to a transparent sheen, revealing deep networks of multi-colored crystal growths lurking beneath. These naturally occurring phenomena give rise to a kaleidoscopic effect when observed from afar.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:68`

## extreme_frontiers.160.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.160.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.160.desc.default$\n\n虽然它具有传统的美学价值，但这里丰富的矿藏才是我们关注的真正目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.160.desc.default$\n\nWhile conventionally aesthetically pleasing, the rich mineral deposits that lie within are the real target of our attention.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:69`

## PLASMA_VOLCANO_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APLASMA_VOLCANO_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道等离子体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

Orbital Plasma

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:80`

## plasma_volcano_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplasma_volcano_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们目睹了敏感的超热等离子体喷发到§Y[from.GetName]§!的上层大气中。\n\n大气层中浓厚的火山灰让我们无法从轨道上辨识这些喷射源，但近距离研究似乎存在危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

We have visual confirmation of volatile superheated plasma being launched into the upper atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThick volcanic ash in the atmosphere is preventing us from identifying the source of these projectiles from orbit. Closer study will likely be dangerous.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:81`

## extreme_frontiers.170.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.170.title&lang=en)

### 中文

等离子火山

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Plasma Volcanoes

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:82`

## extreme_frontiers.170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!发现了§Y[from.GetName]§!上的这些超热等离子喷流的源头。\n\n这颗行星的独特磁场促进了地幔深处电离物质之间的反应，形成了高压等离子体的熔岩室。\n\n行星上不稳定的构造板块坍塌时，这些等离子体就像致命的炮弹一样向上射出，与大气中的气体一起点燃，产生二次爆炸。我们的武器专家热衷于将这些原理应用于自己的等离子武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

§Y[Leader.GetName]§! has uncovered the cause of the superheated plasma eruptions on §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThe unique magnetic field of the planet facilitates a reaction between ionized materials deep within the planetary mantle, creating volcanic chambers of highly pressurized plasma.\n\nWhen the unstable tectonic plates of the planet give way, this plasma is explosively propelled upward as a deadly projectile, igniting with gases in the atmosphere to create secondary explosions. Our weapons specialists are keen to apply these principles to our own plasma-based armaments.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:83`

## extreme_frontiers.170.desc.negative

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.170.desc.negative&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，这项高度危险的研究给[Leader.GetName]留下了严重的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Unfortunately, the hazardous nature of this research has left [Leader.GetName] with severe

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:84`

## BLACK_CLOUDS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABLACK_CLOUDS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

黑云

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

Black Clouds

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:89`

## black_clouds_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_clouds_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[from.GetName]§!的大气中发现了浓厚的黑云，它们无视行星的风速和风向以极不正常的方式运动着。这些黑云只在我们的成像扫描中短暂出现，并且总是在行星昼夜周期的同一时间点上出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Thick black clouds have been observed in §Y[from.GetName]'s§! atmosphere, moving in erratic patterns independent of planetary wind speed or direction. They appear only briefly on our imaging scans and always at the same point in the planet's day-night cycle.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:90`

## extreme_frontiers.180.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.180.title&lang=en)

### 中文

类昆虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

Insectoid Swarms

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:91`

## extreme_frontiers.180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!异常的黑云事实上是某种大型食肉昆虫的密集虫群。\n\n这些长着翅膀的掠食者在岩石裂缝中筑巢，每天定时从中倾巢而出，吞吃星球表面所剩无几的有机物。\n\n得益于强大的新陈代谢可以在在进食时逆转细胞衰变，使得这些节肢动物只要定期消耗营养就能无限期地延长寿命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

The unusual black clouds on §Y[from.GetName]§! are actually dense swarms of large carnivorous insects.\n\nThese winged predators nest in rock fissures and emerge once daily to devour any scant organic matter available on the planet's surface.\n\nThanks to a potent metabolism which reverses cell decay upon feeding, these arthropods can prolong their lifespan indefinitely, as long as nutrients are consumed regularly.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:92`

## extreme_frontiers.180.desc.altered_metabolism

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.180.desc.altered_metabolism&lang=en)

### 中文

在田野调查分析的过程中，[Leader.GetName]的[Leader.Species.GetHandName]被其中的一个标本咬伤，下a额触发了一种显著的代谢响应。这种独特的物种身上还有很多关于有机细胞再生能力的知识可以学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

During fieldwork analysis, [Leader.GetName]'s [Leader.Species.GetHandName] was bitten by one of the specimens, triggering a remarkable metabolic response. This unique species has much to teach us about the regenerative capacities of organic cells.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:93`

## extreme_frontiers.180.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.180.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把它们全部挖出来用掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

Unearth and consume them all.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:94`

## SUBGLACIAL_VIBRATIONS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASUBGLACIAL_VIBRATIONS_CAT&lang=en)

冰下振动

### English

Subglacial Vibrations

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:99`

## subglacial_vibrations_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubglacial_vibrations_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的冰冻荒原之下潜藏着巨大的冰下海洋。在深深的永冻层下探测到的隆隆震动，从本质上无法被归类为地震。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Beneath §Y[from.GetName]'s§! frozen and lifeless surface lies a vast subglacial ocean. Deep, rumbling vibrations have been detected beneath the permafrost that are not registering as seismic in nature.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:100`

## extreme_frontiers.190.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.190.title&lang=en)

### 中文

鳍脚巨兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Gigantic Pinnipeds

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:101`

## extreme_frontiers.190.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.190.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

深海回声定位和地震遥感相结合定位出了§Y[from.GetName]§!深层振动的源头。其液态氮构成的冰下海洋是一大群巨型鳍脚类动物的家园，它们已经适应了如此严苛的生存环境。\n\n这种引人注目的生物无论是尺寸还是重量都与一艘小型运输舰相当。他们发出强力的低频嗡嗡声来进行交流，这种声音甚至可以在整个行星范围内回荡。一种独特的富含胶原蛋白的鲸脂保护着它们免受低温烧伤和霜冻，同时还能在海洋中放大它们的叫声。\n\n这些巨型动物在这个孤立的生态系统中没有任何天敌，它们非常温顺，通过重复它们独特的声音很容易就能吸引到洋面上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

A combination of deep sea echolocation and seismic telemetry has located the source of the deep vibrations on §Y[from.GetName]§!. Its subglacial liquid nitrogen ocean is home to vast herds of gigantic pinnipeds who have adapted to life in this harsh environment.\n\nThe remarkable creatures have the size and mass of a small transport craft. To communicate, they emit a powerful low-frequency drone that can reverberate on a planetwide scale. A uniquely collagen-rich blubber protects them from cryogenic burns and frostbite, while at the same time amplifying their calls across the sea.\n\nLacking any natural predators in this isolated ecosystem, these large animals are curious, highly docile, and can be easily drawn to the surface by replicating their idiosyncratic vocalizations.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:102`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.190.a&lang=en)

独特的发现。

### English

一座为从[event_target:pinnipeds_home_planet.GetName]的液氮海洋中捕获的巨大鳍脚类动物建立的保护区。

A sanctuary for gigantic pinnipeds captured in the liquid nitrogen oceans of [event_target:pinnipeds_home_planet.GetName].

## ELECTRIC_WATERS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AELECTRIC_WATERS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

带电海洋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

Electrical Waters

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:110`

## electric_waters_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_waters_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的轨道扫描检测到这颗行星深深的冰洋之下有异常的电信号。\n\n对这种水中自然现象进行进一步研究可以获得更多发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Orbital scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! are detecting erratic electrical signals deep within the planet's icy seas.\n\nFurther study into this aquatic phenomenon will reveal more.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:111`

## extreme_frontiers.200.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.200.title&lang=en)

### 中文

高压珊瑚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

High Voltage Coral

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:112`

## extreme_frontiers.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Leader.GetName]§!找到了§Y[from.GetName]§!上深层海洋电流的来源。\n\n这片海底是一种具有发电能力的复杂冷水珊瑚物种的家园。该物种使用独特的细胞器产生电流来吸引微生物，亦作为一种捕食和交流的手段。\n\n我们的探测器对珊瑚有刺激作用，只要我们改变传输频率，珊瑚就会做出反应，提高电流输出。\n\n仔细调节与这种深水物种的互动，我们就可以在不破坏脆弱生态系统的前提下增加星球的能源输出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

The source of the deep sea electrical currents on §Y[from.GetName]§! has been identified by §Y[Leader.GetName]§!.\n\nThe ocean floor is home to a complex species of cold-water coral with electrogenetic capabilities. This species uses specialized organelles to generate current to lure microorganisms as both a means of sustenance and communication.\n\nOur survey probes are acting as a stimulant for the coral, which reacts by increasing its electrical output whenever we change a transmission frequency.\n\nBy carefully modulating our interactions with this deep water species, we can increase the planet's energy output without disrupting the delicate ecosystem.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:113`

## OBSIDIAN_PLANET_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOBSIDIAN_PLANET_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

闪光地景

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Glistening Landscape

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:116`

## obsidian_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!进行视觉扫描发现黑曜岩大陆板块覆盖着这颗行星贫瘠的表面，上层大气中残留着大量的硫。\n\n专项地质调查将让我们得到更多结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Visual scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! show continental sheets of obsidian coating the barren surfaces of the planet, with heavy traces of sulfur lingering in the upper atmosphere.\n\nA more concentrated geological survey will tell us more.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:117`

## extreme_frontiers.210.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.210.title&lang=en)

### 中文

黑曜石荒原

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Obsidian Wasteland

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:118`

## extreme_frontiers.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

基于对§Y[from.GetName]§!沉积物的研究，我们发现这颗星球曾经遍布火山，却遭到了骤冷事件，其表面的熔岩变成了成片成片的火山玻璃。\n\n行星表面无数的撞击坑表明该事件可能是由于外源天体，例如彗星风暴的撞击导致的。在这些黑曜石结构的内部是巨大的空洞和因骤冷事件被从火山口捕获其中的含硫气体。\n\n通过精准的提取我们就能收获这些资源为自己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Based on sedimentary analysis of §Y[from.GetName]§!, we have discovered that this once volcanic world experienced a sudden flash freezing event, turning its surface lava into a sheen of volcanic glass.\n\nThe many impact craters on the planet's surface suggest this was most likely caused by an external celestial impact, such as a meteor storm. Contained within these obsidian formations are large bubbles of trapped sulfuric gases from the volcanic vents now sealed by the flash freeze.\n\nWith precise extraction, we will be able to harvest these resources for our own needs.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:119`

## SHEARING_FORCE_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHEARING_FORCE_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

失去联络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Lost Communications 

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:122`

## shearing_force_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashearing_force_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

释放到§Y[from.GetName]§!大气层中的探测器停止了一切通讯。我们失去了该硬件的行踪。\n\n需要对该行星进行近距离调查才能取回这些数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

Observation probes deployed into the atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§! have ceased all communications. The exact location of our hardware is now unknown.\n\nA closer investigation of the planet is needed to recover the data.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:123`

## extreme_frontiers.220.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.220.title&lang=en)

### 中文

剪切力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Shearing Force

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:124`

## extreme_frontiers.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到了§Y[from.GetName]§!上探测器失踪的原因。\n\n构成这颗行星大气的各气体层之间表现出巨大的温差。气体层剧烈摩擦时，这种剧变就产生了强烈的逆流。\n\n这一点，再加上每种气体密度的差异，形成了灾难性的剪切力，让任何穿过大气圈层的物体都被无情撕碎。\n\n既然我们了解了这种现象，就可以改造设备使其能承受这种极端条件，从而利用这种自然现象的动能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

We have identified the cause of our probes' disappearance on §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThe various gaseous strata that make up the planet's atmosphere exhibit dramatic temperature differentials. These drastic variations create a sharp countercurrent as layers of gas move violently in opposing directions.\n\nThis, combined with the contrasting densities of each gas, fosters cataclysmic shearing forces that tear apart anything transitioning between the atmospheric layers.\n\nNow that we know what to expect, our own equipment can be modified to endure these extreme conditions, letting us harness the kinetic force of this natural phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:125`

## MISSING_MASS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMISSING_MASS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

缺失质量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Missing Mass

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:128`

## missing_mass_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amissing_mass_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的质量读数远低于一颗同等尺寸岩质行星预期范围。建议进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

Mass readings for §Y[from.GetName]§! are well below the expected range for a rocky planet of this size. Further investigation is recommended.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:129`

## extreme_frontiers.230.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.230.title&lang=en)

### 中文

深地幔环节动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

Deep Mantle Annelids

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:130`

## extreme_frontiers.230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的地理进行进一步探测发现其硅酸盐地幔中存在一个巨大的地下网络。\n\n探测器显示，这些洞穴居住着一种生活在硅酸盐岩石内的深地幔环节动物。大量的这种生物从内部吞噬着星球，然后将消化的废物喷射到行星轨道上，被宇宙风剥离。\n\n消灭这些环节动物可以让我们的[controller.GetMinerPlural_lower]获得大量高品易得的硅酸盐矿石。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Closer inspection of §Y[from.GetName]'s§! geology found that its silicate mantle features a sprawling network of large subterranean burrows.\n\nExploratory probes revealed that these hollows are home to a thriving species of deep mantle annelid which subsists on silicate rock. Vast colonies of these creatures are devouring the planet from within, then ejecting the digested waste into planetary orbit where it is stripped away by cosmic winds.\n\nExterminating the annelids would grant our [controller.GetMinerPlural_lower] access to extensive quantities of easily accessible silicate ore.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:131`

## extreme_frontiers.230.desc.terravore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.230.desc.terravore&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.230.desc$它们的饮食非常引人注目，与我们相差无几。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.230.desc$ Their appetite is impressive, and not so different from ours.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:132`

## extreme_frontiers.230.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.230.a&lang=en)

### 中文

启动钻机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Fire up the drills.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:133`

## extreme_frontiers.230.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.230.b&lang=en)

### 中文

研究这些奇妙的生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

Study these fascinating creatures.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:134`

## LIFE_IN_DARKNESS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALIFE_IN_DARKNESS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

黑夜生灵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Life in Darkness

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:137`

## life_in_darkness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alife_in_darkness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!被潮汐锁定了，有一半永远处于黑夜之中。红外扫描发现这片漆黑贫瘠的区域竟是某种活跃的有机生态系统所在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! is tidally locked with one half of the planet existing in a state of permanent darkness. Infrared scans indicate that this lightless and nutrient deficient zone is home to an active organic ecosystem.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:138`

## extreme_frontiers.240.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.240.title&lang=en)

### 中文

半透明演化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Translucent Evolution

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:139`

## extreme_frontiers.240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的永夜半球形成了一种独特的演化环境，让少数有机体能够生存在这颗严酷的行星上。\n\n得益于独特的遗传特性，这里的生物群全都是半透明的，它们没有任何视觉，赖以非常复杂的回声定位生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The permanently dark side of §Y[from.GetName]§! has fostered a unique evolutionary environment for the few organisms able to survive this harsh world.\n\nThanks to a unique chromosomal variation, the fauna here are entirely translucent, possess no vision, and deploy sophisticated forms of echolocation to survive.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:140`

## extreme_frontiers.240.desc.non_homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.240.desc.non_homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.240.desc$这种独特的适应性让我们的[controller.GetResearcherPlural_lower]们颇感兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.240.desc$ These remarkable adaptations are of great interest to our [controller.GetResearcherPlural_lower].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:141`

## RAINBOW_RIVERS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARAINBOW_RIVERS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

荧光之河

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

Fluorescent Rivers

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:147`

## rainbow_rivers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arainbow_rivers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的初步扫描发现，遍流行星表面的荧光河不停地在整个光谱中变化。\n\n进一步的研究会加深我们对这个异常自然现象的了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Preliminary scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! show fluorescent rivers flowing across the planet's surface that continuously shift through the full range of the color spectrum.\n\nFurther study will tell us more about this unusual phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:148`

## extreme_frontiers.250.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.250.title&lang=en)

### 中文

多彩化学合成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Colorful Chemosynthesis

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:149`

## extreme_frontiers.250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!上荧光河流的分析发现其中居住着某种独特的微生物。\n\n这些生物利用化学合成作用来代谢有害物质，并产生高度反应性的副产物。这些副产物改变河流的化学构成，造就了这种强烈的变色效果。\n\n它们发出的充满活力的霓虹灯色调同时也是一种交流手段，用以表明它们的生育状况，在穿越星球上有毒溪流的同时吸引配偶。\n\n继续研究这些生物及其生命周期可以为我们的[controller.GetResearcherPlural_lower]们带来不俗的研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

Analysis of the fluorescent rivers on §Y[from.GetName]§! has revealed they are home to a unique species of microbial life.\n\nUsing chemosynthesis to metabolize harmful compounds, these creatures generate highly reactive byproducts that alter the river's chemical composition, causing dramatic color shifts.\n\nDoubling as a means of communication, the vibrant neon hues they create are used to signal fertility status, attracting mates as they travel through the planet's toxic streams.\n\nContinued study of these creatures and their lifecycle will provide a fascinating research opportunity for our [controller.GetResearcherPlural_lower].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:150`

## GASEOUS_METALS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGASEOUS_METALS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

气态金属

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Gaseous Metals

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:153`

## gaseous_metals_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agaseous_metals_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的大气主要由各种气态金属构成。进一步了解这颗行星的严苛条件后我们或许就能提取这些有价值的化合物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

The atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§! is primarily composed of various metals in a gaseous state. With better understanding of this planet's harsh conditions we may be able to extract these valuable compounds.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:154`

## extreme_frontiers.260.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.260.title&lang=en)

### 中文

蒸镀云

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Electroplating Clouds

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:155`

## extreme_frontiers.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]的§!异常大气捕获了大量的恒星辐射，导致行星暖化的连锁效应，甚至连金属都转化为高度活泼的气态形式存在。\n\n这些化合物会与我们投放的所有调查设备瞬间发生反应，在表面附上一层结实的合金薄膜。而这一过程竟然不具有破坏性。\n\n§Y[leader.GetName]§!相信这一自然存在的蒸镀过程可以利用来提升§Y[controller.GetName]§!内所有舰船船体的抗拉强度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]'s§! unusual atmosphere has trapped vast amounts of solar radiation, causing a cascade of planetary warming such that metals exist in a gaseous and highly volatile state.\n\nThese compounds reacted instantly with any survey equipment we deployed, coating it in a thin but durable metallic alloy layer. Surprisingly, this process was non-destructive.\n\n§Y[leader.GetName]§! believes this naturally-occurring electroplating process could be harnessed to improve the tensile strength of ship hulls across the §Y[controller.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:156`

## ATMOSPHERIC_ELECTROPLATING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AATMOSPHERIC_ELECTROPLATING&lang=en)

### 中文

大气蒸镀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Atmospheric Electroplating

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:157`

## ATMOSPHERIC_ELECTROPLATING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AATMOSPHERIC_ELECTROPLATING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

将物体送入§Y[from.GetName]§!的气态金属大气中可以形成天然镀层。\n\n这一独特的过程非常高效，通过进一步研究可以进行复现并用于改进[Root.Owner.GetName]所有舰船船体的耐用度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Sending objects into the gaseous metal atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§! results in a form of natural electroplating.\n\nThis unique process is remarkably effective and with further study could be replicated to improve ship hull durability across the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:158`

## extreme_frontiers.265.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.265.title&lang=en)

### 中文

高性能金属

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

High Performance Metal

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:159`

## extreme_frontiers.265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到工业规模复现§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上活性大气的特殊蒸镀过程的方法。\n\n利用我们学到的知识，[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]们就能大幅提高特定金属的耐用性而不需要牺牲韧性或增加重量。\n\n这些先进的方法可以应用到我们的舰船建造流程中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

We have found a way to replicate the unusual electroplating process of §Y[from.From.GetName]'s§! volatile atmosphere on an industrial scale.\n\nUsing what we have learned, our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] can now greatly increase the durability of certain metals without sacrificing flexibility or adding weight.\n\nThese advanced methods can be applied to our ship construction processes.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:160`

## CARBON_ALLOTROPES_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACARBON_ALLOTROPES_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

碳的同素异形体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Carbon Allotropes

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:163`

## carbon_allotropes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarbon_allotropes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的波谱信号表明行星上的一种成分富含某种稀有而复杂的碳同素异形体。为了更彻底的分类，需要更仔细的检查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

Spectroscopy data from §Y[from.GetName]§! indicates a planetary composition rich in rare and complex carbon allotropes. Closer inspection is required for more thorough categorization.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:164`

## extreme_frontiers.270.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.270.title&lang=en)

### 中文

石墨烯合成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

Graphene Formations

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:165`

## extreme_frontiers.270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[from.GetName]§!上发现了丰富的石墨烯矿藏。这种碳的稀有同素异形体仅有一个分子厚，却拥有无与伦比的强度和导电性。\n\n这种高价值的纳米材料在其地表自然形成，主要得益于这颗§Y碳星球§!上独特大气的化学蒸气沉积。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Abundant deposits of graphene have been found on §Y[from.GetName]§!. This rare allotrope of carbon is only a single molecule thick but possesses unparalleled strength and conductivity.\n\nThe valuable nanomaterial forms naturally on the surface here thanks to chemical vapor deposition inherent to this §YCarbon World's§! unusual atmosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:166`

## extreme_frontiers.270.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.270.a&lang=en)

### 中文

提取出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Extract it.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:167`

## DIAMOND_DUST_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADIAMOND_DUST_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

高速物质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

High Velocity Mass

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:170`

## diamond_dust_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adiamond_dust_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的质量扫描发现对流层下层有大量快速移动的物质。\n\n如果没有更详细的调查，这些异常读数的来源很难加以确认。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

Mass scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! reveal high concentrations of fast moving physical matter located deep within the lower troposphere.\n\nThe source of these unusual readings is difficult to verify without further detailed investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:171`

## extreme_frontiers.280.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.280.title&lang=en)

### 中文

钻石风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Diamond Storms

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:172`

## extreme_frontiers.280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们辨明了§Y[from.GetName]§!上不同寻常的质量读数的来源。\n\n这颗行星的大气主要由富含碳的甲烷气体组成，随着重力压力的增加，甲烷气体会固化成超致密的钻石结构。由于行星上剧烈的风暴周期，于是就产生了快速移动的钻石风暴。\n\n尽管这些钻石很容易提取为我们所用，但§Y[leader.GetName]§!相信进一步研究其密度和纯度会得出更有价值的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

We have identified the source of the unusual mass readings on §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThe planet's atmosphere is composed primarily of carbon-rich methane gas, which solidifies into ultra-dense diamond structures as gravitational pressure increases. Due to the planet's fierce storm cycles, the phenomenon gives rise to fast-moving diamond storms.\n\nWhile these diamonds are easy to extract for our own needs, §Y[leader.GetName]§! is confident that closer examination of their density and purity will yield further valuable results.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:173`

## ATMOSPHERIC_DIAMONDS_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AATMOSPHERIC_DIAMONDS_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

大气钻石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Atmospheric Diamonds

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:174`

## ATMOSPHERIC_DIAMONDS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AATMOSPHERIC_DIAMONDS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

研究§Y[from.GetName]§!上的超密度钻石后，我们确信其独特性质可以用于发展我们的激光科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

By studying the ultra-dense diamond storms on §Y[from.GetName]§!, we are confident that their unique properties can be used to advance our laser technologies.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:175`

## extreme_frontiers.285.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.285.title&lang=en)

### 中文

钻石精准

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Diamond Precision

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:176`

## extreme_frontiers.285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!对§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上超密钻石的研究完成了。\n\n形成这些钻石的压缩过程产生了独特的晶格结构，使其在限制和放大高频光振荡方面非常有效。\n\n这一突破将为我们的帝国带来极大的好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§!'s research into the ultra-dense diamonds of §Y[from.From.GetName]§! has concluded.\n\nThe compression process that creates these diamonds results in a unique crystalline lattice structure. This renders them remarkably efficient at confining and amplifying high-frequency light oscillation.\n\nThis breakthrough will prove an excellent boon for our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:177`

## GEOLOGICAL_TENSION_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEOLOGICAL_TENSION_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

极端地质应力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Extreme Geological Tension

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:180`

## geological_tension_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ageological_tension_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的地质扫描表明这颗熔融星球的地壳承受着非常巨大的构造应力。可能有重大的地质事件即将发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Geological scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! suggest that the planetary crust of this molten world is under immense pressure from pent-up tectonic forces. A significant geological event may be imminent.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:181`

## extreme_frontiers.290.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.290.title&lang=en)

### 中文

受阻超级火山

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Blocked Supervolcano

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:182`

## extreme_frontiers.290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的极端地质应力进行深入研究之后，我们发现了一座巨大的超级火山因上部火山锥的部分崩塌而被堵塞。\n\n大量滞留的熔岩已经灌满了所有能联通的底下空间，正在对主通喷口的堵塞物施加巨大的向上压力。坍塌物暂时将岩浆禁锢在原地，但谁也不知道这种微妙的平衡还能持续多久。\n\n被锁住的熔岩含有异常多的稀有金属和多种矿物质。§Y[leader.GetName]§!相信我们帮助它可控喷发之后就可以收获这些资源了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

After further study of the extreme seismic tension on §Y[from.GetName]§!, we have discovered that a colossal supervolcano has become blocked by a partial upper cone collapse.\n\nVast reserves of magma have filled all available underground chambers and are exerting immense upward pressure against the blockage in the main vent. The collapsed material is currently holding the magma in place, but it is impossible to predict how long this delicate equilibrium will last.\n\nThe trapped molten rock is exceptionally rich in rare metals and complex mineral compounds. §Y[leader.GetName]§! believes we can harvest these resources safely by facilitating a controlled eruption.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:183`

## extreme_frontiers.290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.290.a&lang=en)

### 中文

疏通喷口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Unblock the vent.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:184`

## extreme_frontiers.290.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.290.b&lang=en)

### 中文

充分利用这种地质活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Put the geothermal activity to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:185`

## CONTROLLED_ERUPTION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACONTROLLED_ERUPTION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

受控喷发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Controlled Eruption

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:186`

## CONTROLLED_ERUPTION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACONTROLLED_ERUPTION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上的一座受阻的超级火山之下储存着超大量富矿熔岩。进行受控喷发之后我们就可以收获这些被阻碍的资源了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

A blocked supervolcano on §Y[from.GetName]§! is holding vast reserves of mineral-rich magma below the surface. Conducting a controlled eruption would allow us to harvest these trapped resources.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:187`

## extreme_frontiers.295.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.295.title&lang=en)

### 中文

史诗级喷发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Mega Eruption

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:188`

## extreme_frontiers.295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[from.From.GetName]§!的超级火山外锥体上的不同位置挖了一系列人工竖井，将其结构削弱，使其无法继续约束下方大量堆积的岩浆。\n\n一越过临界点就会产生惊人的结果。高压的熔岩向上爆炸，摧毁了整座火山的上半部分。\n\n我们将溢出的熔岩引导到精心准备的通道中，以专门的采矿设备进行收集。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

By boring a series of artificial shafts at various points in the outer cone of §Y[from.From.GetName]'s§! supervolcano, we weakened its structure until it was no longer able to contain the massive accumulation of magma below.\n\nOnce the tipping point was reached, the results were spectacular. The volcano's upper section was obliterated entirely as highly pressured molten rock exploded upward.\n\nWe directed the overflowing lava into carefully prepared channels, where it will be harvested by specialized mining equipment.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:189`

## extreme_frontiers.295.a.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.295.a.default&lang=en)

叹为观止。

### English

Spectacular.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALAYERED_ATMOSPHERE_CAT&lang=en)

喷发巨行星

### English

Erupting Giant

## layered_atmosphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alayered_atmosphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的扫描发现可燃气体在整颗行星上零星喷发，给远程观测带来了挑战。\n\n需要进行近距离调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

Scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! have detected sporadic eruptions of combustible gases across the planet, presenting challenges for remote surveys.\n\nA closer inspection will be needed.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:195`

## extreme_frontiers.300.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.300.title&lang=en)

### 中文

分层大气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

Layered Atmosphere

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:196`

## extreme_frontiers.300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了§Y[from.GetName]§!上剧烈喷发的源头。\n\n这颗气态巨行星的大气的气态中间层和平流层之间悬浮着一层液态金属氢化物。下层密度更高的甲烷气体托举着这些液体漂浮。\n\n随着时间的推移，释放的电荷在金属氢内部积聚，扰动亚层。在压力达到临界点后，气体向上层大气中喷出高导电性液体，弥散到整颗行星之后重新沉降下来。\n\n我们的追踪系统可以轻易显示这些压力波动，方便我们收获这些高价值资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! has uncovered the source of the volatile eruptions on §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThe atmosphere of this gas giant has formed in such a way that a layer of liquid metallic hydrogen is suspended between the gaseous mesosphere and stratosphere. The denser composition of methane gas in the lower layer keeps the fluid aloft.\n\nOver time, electrical discharges build up within the metallic hydrogen, agitating the sublayer. After a critical build up of pressure, gaseous eruptions spew out highly conductive liquid into the upper atmosphere, dissipating across the planet before resettling.\n\nOur tracking systems can easily monitor these pressure fluctuations, allowing us to harvest these valuable resources.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:197`

## PLASMA_TRAILS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APLASMA_TRAILS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

能量波动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

Erratic Surges

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:200`

## plasma_trails_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplasma_trails_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沿§Y[from.GetName]§!的构造断层线探测到了不稳定的能量波动。\n\n这些波动的根源无法从轨道上加以确定。需要近距离研究才能弄清该现象的源头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

Erratic energy surges are being detected along the tectonic fault lines of §Y[from.GetName]§!.\n\nThe origin of these spikes cannot be ascertained from orbit. Closer study will be required to identify the source of this phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:201`

## extreme_frontiers.310.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.310.title&lang=en)

### 中文

固化等离子航迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

Solidified Plasma Trails

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:202`

## extreme_frontiers.310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的地质分析找到了之前在轨道上发现的异常能量波动的源头。\n\n这颗行星活跃的地质活动在冻土不稳定的地区产生了剧烈的静电放电。\n\n这种火山闪电向天空爆发，将周围的空气电离。这一事件释放的极端热量形成热冲击，然后固化成闪电状的结构。\n\n利用地质分析获得的知识，我们可以轻易地预测这些地震爆发，并对其加以利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

Geological analysis of §Y[from.GetName]§! has identified the source of the erratic energy spikes detected during initial orbital surveys.\n\nThe planet's volatile tectonic activity produces violent electrostatic discharges in regions with unstable frozen tundra.\n\nThis volcanic lightning erupts skywards turning the surrounding air to plasma. The extreme heat unleashed by this event produces a thermal shock which then solidifies into lightning-shaped structures.\n\nUsing what we have learned from the geological analysis, we will be able to easily predict these seismic eruptions and exploit this phenomenon for our own purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:203`

## CRYSTALLINE_PLAINS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACRYSTALLINE_PLAINS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

冷火山活动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Cryovolcanic Activity

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:206`

## crystalline_plains_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystalline_plains_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的地质分析在全星球发现了性质异常的显著冰火山活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Tectonic analysis of §Y[from.GetName]§! shows significant cryovolcanic activity across the planet of an anomalous nature.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:207`

## extreme_frontiers.320.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.320.title&lang=en)

### 中文

晶体平原

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Crystalline Plains

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:208`

## extreme_frontiers.320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!进行细致的地理学调查发现在冷冻的地壳下流动着半凝固的氨河流。提高地热压力将驱动这些物质穿过脆弱表面的裂隙。\n\n这些喷发口遍布行星表面，射出的危险蒸汽和氨泥浆的敏感混合物。当暴露在极端的表面低温后瞬间冻结，这些烂泥形成了遍布易采收高价值晶体的广阔平原。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Detailed geological surveys of §Y[from.GetName]§! have revealed rivers of semi-frozen ammonia flowing beneath its frozen crust. Rising geothermal pressures drive this substance through fissures in the brittle surface.\n\nThese eruptions spew a volatile mixture of hazardous vapors and ammonia slush across the surface of the planet. Refreezing as it becomes exposed to the extreme surface temperatures, the slush forms sprawling plains of easily harvestable and valuable crystals.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:209`

## SUPERMASSIVE_GIANT_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASUPERMASSIVE_GIANT_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

超大质量巨行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Supermassive Giant

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:212`

## supermassive_giant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asupermassive_giant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!是一颗超大质量气态巨行星，其强大的引力井将数千物体吸引到其轨道上。将其进行分类编目应该会很有价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! is a supermassive gas giant whose strong gravity well has swept thousands of objects into its orbit. Cataloging them may prove worthwhile.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:213`

## extreme_frontiers.330.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.330.title&lang=en)

### 中文

太空垃圾

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Space Litter

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:214`

## extreme_frontiers.330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!将无数的小卫星、小行星和太空碎片约束在其轨道上。放射性同位素定年表明这些东西都是在数千年前路过该恒星系的时候被它的引力井捕获的。\n\n在对这些物体进行编目的过程中我们发现了一些古老而原始的探测器，表明存在一个正在初涉太空探索的文明。\n\n其中大多数数据库都已经腐坏或因与其他物体撞击而损毁。但其中一个包含有一系列[from.GetName]地表的图像，时间跨度长达数百年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! has a multitude of moonlets, asteroids, and space debris locked within its orbit. Radioactive isotope dating suggests they were all captured by the planet's gravity well while passing through the system thousands of years ago.\n\nWhile cataloging these objects we found a few ancient and very basic survey probes, suggestive of a civilization making its first forays in space exploration.\n\nMost of their data banks are either corrupted or physically damaged from collisions with other objects, but one contained a series of images of [from.GetName]'s surface spanning hundreds of years.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:215`

## extreme_frontiers.330.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.330.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有意思的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

A fascinating find.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:216`

拆了它。

### English

Scrap them.

## INVISIBLE_PLANET_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINVISIBLE_PLANET_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道不规则

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Orbit Irregularity

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:220`

## invisible_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainvisible_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的轨道路径上有一个反复出现的微小震荡。这表明它有规律地受到某种大型未知引力的影响。\n\n建议对其周边环境进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

A small but recurring oscillation has been observed in the orbit path of §Y[from.GetName]§!, suggesting it is regularly subjected to some large unknown gravitational force.\n\nFurther study of the surrounding space is recommended.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:221`

## extreme_frontiers.340.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.340.title&lang=en)

### 中文

不可见行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Invisible Planet

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:222`

## extreme_frontiers.340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§!的进一步分析发现该恒星系中所有的行星公转轨道都呈现出了相似的异常。\n\n在利用异常的运动对重力源进行三角定位后，我们发现了个中缘由：一颗此前没有发现的行星，现在我们将其命名为§Y[invisible_planet.GetName]§!。\n\n这颗星球表面拥有独特的炭黑颗粒，完全不反射可见光和无线电波，使其对长程扫描完全隐身。\n\n这些天然产生的聚合物具有绝佳的吸收性能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Further analysis of §Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§! found that all planetary orbits in this system exhibit similar irregularities.\n\nAfter triangulating the gravitational forces implied by these unusual movements, we discovered their cause: a previously unseen planet now known as §Y[invisible_planet.GetName]§!.\n\nThis world possesses a unique surface of carbon black particles which reflect no light or radio waves, making it virtually invisible to long-range scanners.\n\nThese naturally occurring polymers have remarkably absorbent properties.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:223`

## extreme_frontiers.340.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.340.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.340.desc$这些物质具有独特的研究价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.340.desc$ These materials present a unique research opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:224`

## extreme_frontiers.340.desc.first_contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.340.desc.first_contact&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.340.desc$我们将对其进行采收并研究其作为隐身材料的潜在价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.340.desc$ They will be harvested and studied for their potential uses as a stealth material.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:225`

## SURVIVAL_POD_WARM_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASURVIVAL_POD_WARM_CAT&lang=en)

改造的外勤站

### English

Repurposed Field Station

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:228`

## SURVIVAL_POD_COLD_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASURVIVAL_POD_COLD_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

改造的外勤站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Repurposed Field Station

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:229`

## survival_pod_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asurvival_pod_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!贫瘠地表的勘测显示出一个被改造的外勤站，这最初只是一座临时研究实验室，如今早已超出预期寿命。微弱的热辐射表明，该设施的部分区域依然处于活跃状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Survey sweeps of §Y[from.GetName]'s§! barren surface have revealed a repurposed field station, originally a temporary research lab long past its intended lifespan. Faint heat radiation suggest parts of the installation remain active.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:230`

## extreme_frontiers.350.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.350.title&lang=en)

### 中文

孤独幸存者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Lone Survivor

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:231`

## extreme_frontiers.350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!进行的地表探险发现了这一改造的外勤站以及它唯一的居住者。\n\n她自称是一名被困多年的科学家，在这片恶劣的星球里孤身生存至今，如今愿以她的专业知识换取[root.Owner.GetName]的救援。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

A surface expedition to §Y[from.GetName]§! located the repurposed field station and its lone occupant.\n\nIdentifying herself as a stranded scientist who has endured this harsh world for years, she offers her expertise to the [root.Owner.GetName] in exchange for rescue.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:232`

## extreme_frontiers.350.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.350.a&lang=en)

### 中文

她的经验可是无价之宝。

### English

Her experience is an asset.

### English

Gift her a shuttle and supplies.

别理她，继续观测。

### English

Leave her be and observe.

## extreme_frontiers.350.d

真有趣。摧毁她的庇护所。

### English

Adorable. Destroy her shelter.
