## extreme_frontiers.352.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.title&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_survivalist_scru$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

$NAME_survivalist_scru$

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:237`

## extreme_frontiers.352.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位形容枯槁的[scru_species.GetClass]走出她改装为居所的外勤站。当她谈及自己在§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上的遭遇时，从她[scru_species.GetMouthName]中发出的声音沙哑破碎；言语断断续续。\n\n“请原谅……我已经很多年……没有和人说过话了……”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

An emaciated [scru_species.GetClass] steps out of her hab-converted field station. When she speaks of her ordeal on §Y[from.From.GetName]§!, the sounds from her [scru_species.GetMouthName] rasps; words arrive in fits and starts.\n\n"Forgive me. I have not... spoken to another in many years..."

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:238`

## extreme_frontiers.352.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么落难到这里了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

What stranded you here?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:239`

## extreme_frontiers.352.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我当时正在勘测[from.From.GetName]，记录基岩的独特特征。我的船员说他们要驾驶探测飞船简单检查一下。我信了他们。焦虑了几天后，我才意识到，他们直接开回家了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

I was surveying [from.From.GetName], cataloging the unique bedrock. My crew said they'd do a quick sweep in our exploration craft. I believed them. Only after a few days of worrying did I realize what had happened. They took off and flew home.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:240`

## extreme_frontiers.352.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你怎么活这么久的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

How did you survive here for so long?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:241`

## extreme_frontiers.352.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

哦，真是奇妙啊。只要稍微改造细胞通透性，再让热休克蛋白过度表达……之后，要在我改造的细胞上诱导大自噬就简单得多了。而多亏了这个世界丰富的钠含量，我得以烹制出许多美味佳肴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Oh it's a wonder what you can do when modifying cell permeability and hyper-expressing heat shock proteins... After that, it was simple to induce macroautophagy on my modified cells, and thanks to the sodium content of this world I could generate many savory meals.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:242`

## extreme_frontiers.352.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你还想和同类们重见吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Do you not want to rejoin your own kind?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:243`

## extreme_frontiers.352.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

他们不配拥有我在这块岩石上取得的突破。我根本不想知道他们抛弃我的神经借口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

They don't deserve the breakthroughs I've made on this rock. I don't care to learn what duplicitous reasons they had for abandoning me.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:244`

## extreme_frontiers.352.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你能为我们做些什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

What can you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:245`

## extreme_frontiers.352.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我已经厌倦在简陋的实验室工作。我渴望从太空去看新世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

I'm tired of working in a makeshift lab. I want to see new worlds... From space.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:246`

## extreme_frontiers.352.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.e&lang=en)

招募$NAME_survivalist_scru$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Enlist $NAME_survivalist_scru$

## extreme_frontiers.352.e.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.352.e.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

§Y$NAME_survivalist_scru$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入我们帝国。

### English

§Y$NAME_survivalist_scru$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Scru

## survivalist_scru_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asurvivalist_scru_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位在极端环境锤炼出求生技能的天赋异禀的科学家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

A talented scientist with survival skills honed in extreme environments.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:250`

## leader_trait_hardened_survivalist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_hardened_survivalist&lang=en)

生存主义老手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Hardened Survivalist

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:251`

## leader_trait_hardened_survivalist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_hardened_survivalist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖拥有的关于极端恶劣环境的知识无与伦比。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

This leader has unparalleled knowledge of inhospitable environments.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:252`

## INFERNAL_TORNADOES_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINFERNAL_TORNADOES_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

极端温度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Extreme Temperatures

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:255`

## infernal_tornadoes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfernal_tornadoes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§H[from.GetName]§!的扫描显示了大面积快速迁移的高压力风暴。\n\n这些风暴释放着极强的热信号。需要着重研究才能揭示该现象是自然发生或是由某种至今未知的生命形式引起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Scans of §H[from.GetName]§! show vast swathes of rapidly migrating high-pressure storms.\n\nThese storms are emitting extremely volatile heat signatures. Focused research will be needed to reveal if the cause is natural or a hitherto unknown form of life.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:256`

## extreme_frontiers.360.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.360.title&lang=en)

### 中文

火龙卷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

Flaming Tornadoes

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:257`

## extreme_frontiers.360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[from.GetName]§!上极端热信号的源头找到了。\n\n遍布行星的间歇泉从地表深处释放出高度易燃的氢气。这些间歇泉喷发时，气体就被行星的热量点燃，产生直抵云霄的巨大火柱。\n\n再和大气压力相结合，就产生了极不稳定的气流，以巨大的火焰龙卷风的形式横扫地面。\n\n尽管这是一种极具破坏力的自然现象，但我们的[Root.Owner.GetResearcherPlural_lower]很确信这些龙卷风以及其产生的大量动能可以人为地复现并加以利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

The source of the extreme heat signatures on §H[from.GetName]§! has been discovered.\n\nAcross the planet, geysers are emitting highly flammable hydrogen gas from deep below the surface. As these geysers erupt, the planet's heat ignites the gas, resulting in vast pillars of flame blasting into the skies.\n\nWhen combined with the atmospheric pressure, this creates extremely volatile air currents, sending streams of fire swirling across the landscape in the form of massive, flaming tornadoes.\n\nWhile a devastating natural phenomena, our [Root.Owner.GetResearcherPlural_lower] are confident that these tornadoes, and the vast volumes of kinetic energy they generate, could be recreated artificially for practical use.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:258`

## INFERNAL_CATALYSTS_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINFERNAL_CATALYSTS_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

炼狱催化剂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

Infernal Catalysts

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:259`

## INFERNAL_CATALYSTS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINFERNAL_CATALYSTS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对§H[from.GetName]§!上火龙卷的形成条件更密切的研究，就可以在受控环境中得以复现这种现象好的一面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

By studying the conditions behind the flaming tornadoes of §H[from.GetName]§! more closely, the benefits of this phenomena could be recreated in a controlled environment.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:260`

## extreme_frontiers.365.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.365.title&lang=en)

### 中文

熔岩碎屑共振器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

Pyroclastic Resonators

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:261`

## extreme_frontiers.365.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.365.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Leader.GetName]§!发现了在可控环境中再现并驾驭§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上发现的那种火龙卷的方法。\n\n有了这一突破，我们就能够利用的恒星基地来增加在同一星系中发现的所有熔融星球所产出的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

§Y[from.Leader.GetName]§! has successfully found a way to recreate and harness the flaming tornadoes found on §Y[from.From.GetName]§! in a controlled environment.\n\nWith this breakthrough, we will be able to use our starbases to increase the energy produced by any molten worlds that are found in the same system.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:262`

## extreme_frontiers.365.desc.has_furnace

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.365.desc.has_furnace&lang=en)

### 中文

这项科学理论与我们目前对§H电弧熔炉§!的理解有许多异曲同工之处，打开了域外建筑学领域的许多全新可能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

The scientific theory shares many parallels with our existing understanding of §HArc Furnaces§!, opening new possibilities for offworld construction.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:263`

## extreme_frontiers.365.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.365.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

这一发现背后的科学理论在§H电弧熔炉§!的研发中有诸多应用，我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]已经开始将它们应用在实际开发当中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

The scientific theory behind this discovery has various applications towards the development of §HArc Furnace§! technology, with our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] prepared to start applying the theory in practical development.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:264`

## tech_pyroclastic_resonation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_pyroclastic_resonation&lang=en)

### 中文

熔岩碎屑共振

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Pyroclastic Resonation

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:265`

## tech_pyroclastic_resonation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_pyroclastic_resonation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过驾驭熔融星球上找到的极端热能，我们的恒星基地可以以极高的效率提升恒星系内的能量产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

By harnessing the extreme thermal energy found on molten worlds, our starbases can increase the energy output in a system with exceptional efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:266`

## sm_pyroclastic_resonator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_pyroclastic_resonator&lang=en)

### 中文

熔岩碎屑共振器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

Pyroclastic Resonator

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:267`

## sm_pyroclastic_resonator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_pyroclastic_resonator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可以用于提升恒星系内所有熔融星球能量产出的热共振器。\n$i_energy$与星系内['concept_pc_molten']的数量成正比。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

Thermal resonators that can increase the energy output of any molten world in a star system.\n$i_energy$ production scales with the number of ['concept_pc_molten'] in the system.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:268`

## MAGMA_MEGAFAUNA_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAGMA_MEGAFAUNA_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳地质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Unstable Geography

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:271`

## magma_megafauna_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagma_megafauna_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的扫描显示熔融行星的表面持续不断地在长距离漂移。\n\n需要进行近距离研究才能理解这一异常地理现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

Scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! show sections of the molten planetary surface shifting continuously across large distances.\n\nCloser study is required to understand this unusual geological phenomenon.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:272`

## extreme_frontiers.370.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.370.title&lang=en)

### 中文

熔融大型动物群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Molten Megafauna

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:273`

## extreme_frontiers.370.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.370.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!这些异常的地质扫描结果的根源是一群游荡的硅基巨型动物群。\n\n这些巨大的生物长距离迁徙，周期性地潜入行星的熔岩湖中获取喜欢的食物：一种富含§H易爆微粒§!的岩质沉淀物。通过追踪它们的迁徙路径，我们就能在对行星的生态系统造成极低的影响下提取这些沉淀为自己所用。\n\n它们厚重的外骨骼可以抵御极端温度；对这些生物生命周期进行更详尽深入的研究可以产出惊人的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

The anomalous tectonic scans on §Y[from.GetName]§! have been attributed to the presence of large herds of roaming silicon-based megafauna.\n\nMigrating across great distances, these enormous creatures periodically submerge in the planet's lava lakes to gather their preferred food source: a rocky sediment rich in §HVolatile Motes§!. By tracking their migratory patterns, we will be able to extract these sediments for our own needs with minimal impact to the planet's ecosystem.\n\nTheir thick outer carapaces are able to withstand extreme temperatures; a deeper, more detailed study of their life cycle could yield fascinating results.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:274`

## LAVA_DIVERS_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALAVA_DIVERS_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

潜熔兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Lava Divers

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:275`

## LAVA_DIVERS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALAVA_DIVERS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上的大型动物能抵御熔融星球上的极端环境，深深潜入行星上无数熔岩池之中。\n\n研究这些生物的甲壳可能帮助我们理解如何抵御极端热量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

The megafauna on §Y[from.GetName]§! are able to withstand the harsh environments of their molten world, and submerge themselves deep below the surface of the planet's numerous magma pools.\n\nStudying these creatures' carapaces may give us a new understanding of how to deal with extreme heat.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:276`

## extreme_frontiers.375.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.375.title&lang=en)

### 中文

熔岩锻造装甲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

Magma-forged Armor

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:277`

## extreme_frontiers.375.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.375.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上独特的大型动物进行研究得出了一种可转用的热动力学洞见，能应用在我们的船舶工业中。\n\n这种生物摄入的微粒使他们的外壳上形成了一种基于重质硅酸盐的结构，并得到天然存在的难熔金属的补强。\n\n从它们脱落的外壳上取回无数的样本之后，我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]现在可以复制这种生物力学原理，用于研发我们自己的船体镀层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

Comprehensive study of the unique megafauna on §Y[from.From.GetName]§! has revealed transferable thermodynamic insights which can be applied within our shipyards.\n\nThe mote-infused diet of the creatures results in a heavy silicate-based structure forming throughout their durable outer shells, reinforced with naturally occurring refractory materials.\n\nHaving recovered numerous samples of their shedded outer layers, our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] can now replicate their biomechanical principles in the development of our own hull platings.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:278`

## PLANET_THRUSTERS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APLANET_THRUSTERS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮装置

### English

Suspended Equipment

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:281`

## planet_thrusters_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_thrusters_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的上层大气中悬浮着一系列大型圆柱体机械。其中没有任何操作员，看起来未在运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Suspended within the upper atmosphere of §Y[from.GetName]§! is a series of large cylindrical machines. They appear inactive devoid of any crew.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:282`

## extreme_frontiers.380.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.380.title&lang=en)

### 中文

行星推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

Planet Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:283`

## extreme_frontiers.380.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.380.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上发现的那些遗弃的机器都是一台台独立的推进装置。在大气层中总数超过了一千座。\n\n这些复杂的聚变装置以特殊设计的材料支撑，可以消耗行星下层的氢气作为燃料来产生巨大的推力。其中的大多数上存在过载损伤。\n\n我们的分析表明这些机械的位置都精心安置，使其可以对行星本身施加推力。这表明这些古老的装置最初是为了改变行星的轨道而布置的。\n\n虽然不再适合这个级别的艰巨任务，但我们仍可能重新利用受损较小的装置，燃烧掉星球的气体层，从而暴露出富含矿物质的核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Each of the abandoned machines found on §Y[from.GetName]§! is a standalone thruster component. In total, over one thousand are present in the upper atmosphere.\n\nMade from specially engineered materials, these complex fusion devices are designed to ingest hydrogen gas from the planet below to use as fuel for generating significant amounts of thrust. Most show signs of overload damage.\n\nOur analysis reveals that all of the machines are positioned in such a way that this thrust would exert force against the planet itself, suggesting this ancient hardware was originally deployed in an attempt to alter the planet's orbit.\n\nWhile no longer suitable for this level of monumental task, we may be able to repurpose the lesser damaged models to burn away the planet's gassy layers and expose its mineral-rich core.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:284`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.380.a&lang=en)

全部拆了。

Repurposed Planet Thrusters

## REPURPOSE_THRUSTERS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREPURPOSE_THRUSTERS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过重利用§Y[from.GetName]§!上受损的行星推进器阵列，我们将这颗气态巨行星的氢气焚烧殆尽，暴露出了底下可开采的固态核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

By repurposing the damaged planet thruster array on §Y[from.GetName]§!, we can burn away the gas giant's hydrogen leaving behind a solid and exploitable core.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:288`

## extreme_frontiers.385.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.385.title&lang=en)

### 中文

抽丝剥茧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Gases Stripped

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:289`

## extreme_frontiers.385.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.385.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以修复一部分§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上受损的行星推进装置，将其校准以施加等大反向的推力。\n\n这些强力的聚变装置激活后就能以指数级速率抽取氢气，将行星的整个大气层烧尽，并暴露出其富含矿物的核心，虽然温度可能会高了一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

We were able to repair a few of §Y[from.From.GetName]'s§! damaged planetary thrusters and calibrated them to exert equal force in opposing directions.\n\nOnce activated these powerful fusion devices drew up hydrogen at an exponential rate, burning away the planet's gaseous atmosphere and exposing its mineral rich core, albeit at an extremely high temperature.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:290`

## BACKWARDS_ORBIT_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABACKWARDS_ORBIT_CAT&lang=en)

逆行轨道

### English

Backwards Orbit

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:295`

## backwards_orbit_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abackwards_orbit_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!环绕恒星的方向与恒星系内其他行星相反，这说明它和伴随的天体并不是同时同地形成的。\n\n需要进一步调查来了解该行星的起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]§! orbits its star in the reverse direction compared to the other planets in the system, suggesting it did not form here at the same time as its companion celestial bodies.\n\nFurther investigation is needed to understand this planet's origins.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:296`

## extreme_frontiers.390.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.390.title&lang=en)

### 中文

流浪行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Wandering Planet

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:297`

## extreme_frontiers.390.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.390.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!与其周边的行星的物质组成并不匹配，反倒是和相邻的§Y[event_target:wandering_planet_origin.GetName]§!恒星系中的许多天体完美相符，这暗示了它的真实起源。\n\n这颗行星的表面清晰可见一个硕大无比的撞击坑，这让我们认为它是由于一次大规模的小行星撞击而偏离了原来的轨道。这颗自由漂浮的流浪行星后来被[from.System.Star.GetName]重力井捕获，形成了异常的逆行轨道。\n\n这颗独特的行星从诞生起在两个恒星系中居住过，带来了丰富的深入研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

§Y[from.GetName]'s§! composition does not match that of other planets in its vicinity. It does however align perfectly with various celestial bodies in the neighboring §Y[event_target:wandering_planet_origin.GetName]§! system, suggesting its true origin.\n\nThe planet's surface is marked by a particularly large impact crater, leading us to assume it was dislodged from its original orbit by a massive asteroid collision. This free floating rogue planet was then later captured by the gravity well of [from.System.Star.GetName], where it assumed its unusual reverse orbit.\n\nThis remarkable planet has resided in two different systems since it formed, providing rich opportunities for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:298`

## VAPOR_PLUMES_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AVAPOR_PLUMES_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

羽状蒸汽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Vapor Plumes

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:301`

## vapor_plumes_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avapor_plumes_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[from.GetName]§!的地表上观测到有成千上万的异常蒸汽羽状物升起，延伸到其稀薄大气的顶层。\n\n建议对该现象进行深入调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Thousands of anomalous vapor plumes have been observed rising from the surface of §Y[from.GetName]§!, extending to the upper limits of its thin atmosphere.\n\nFurther investigation of this phenomenon is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:302`

## extreme_frontiers.400.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.400.title&lang=en)

### 中文

冷火山间歇泉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Cryovolcanic Geysers

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:303`

## extreme_frontiers.400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上发现的羽状蒸汽是由下地幔深处强烈的冷火山活动造成的，大量的氨氮气体被排放到地表。\n\n这些羽状云扶摇直上直抵行星的大气层边缘，被恒星风骤然削平。\n\n通过研究这些独特烟雾结构中化学成分的微小变化，我们可以进一步了解行星核心的复杂地质过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

The vapor plumes observed on §Y[from.GetName]§! are caused by intense cryovolcanic activity deep within the lower mantle, expelling vast columns of nitrogen and ammonia gas upwards.\n\nThe plumes rise vertically until they reach the limits of the planet's atmosphere, where solar winds cause them to abruptly flatten off.\n\nBy studying the small variations in the chemical contents of these unique fume formations we can learn more about the complex geological processes of the planet's core.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:304`

## FIBROUS_ATMOSPHERE_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFIBROUS_ATMOSPHERE_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

Corrupted Survey

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:307`

## fibrous_atmosphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afibrous_atmosphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的初步扫描结果是损坏的，我们的仪器无法清晰解读。这一干扰的来源尚不明确；或许需要近距离调查 。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Initial scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! are returning in a corrupted state that our sensors cannot clearly interpret. The source of this interference remains unclear; a closer inspection will be needed.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:308`

## extreme_frontiers.410.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.410.title&lang=en)

### 中文

纤维大气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

Fibrous Atmosphere

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:309`

## extreme_frontiers.410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的详细调查揭示了我们初步扫描时数据损坏的原因。\n\n纤维状矿物形成的浓厚云雾构成了行星的上层大气。这些细长蜿蜒的丝缕源自气态巨行星的下层，高压环境将其拉伸为高导电性的矿物云。\n\n这些纤维一边在大气中循环一边积蓄静电能量，并且会周期性发生放电。我们很容易采收这些资源为自己所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Detailed inspection of §Y[from.GetName]§! has revealed the source of the data corruption in our preliminary scans.\n\nDense clouds of fibrous minerals make up the planet's upper atmosphere. These long sinuous strands originate from the gas giant's lower layers with the high pressure environment stretching them to form highly conductive mineral clouds.\n\nAccumulating electrostatic energy as they circulate through the atmosphere, these fibers periodically discharge over time. We can easily extract these resources for our own needs.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:310`

## EXPLODING_SKIES_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEXPLODING_SKIES_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

强热脉冲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Intense Heat Spikes

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:313`

## exploding_skies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploding_skies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的扫描追踪到了极端的气象现象，地表分布着零星但强烈的热脉冲。\n\n需要进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Scans of §Y[from.GetName]§! are tracking extreme meteorological phenomena, with sporadic but intense heat spikes distributed across the surface.\n\nThis warrants further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:314`

## extreme_frontiers.420.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.420.title&lang=en)

### 中文

爆炸地平线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Explosive Horizon

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:315`

## extreme_frontiers.420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的细致调查发现了气象热脉冲的根源。\n\n星罗棋布的地热喷口和间歇泉不断喷出高度易燃的气体，在大气中逐步累积。静电放电扮演起点燃催化的角色，导致爆炸性的气象锋面席卷整个星球的天空。\n\n这些反应让我们的[Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural]们对热动力燃烧获得了新的洞见，能在我们舰队的推进系统中加以应用

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Detailed observation of §Y[from.GetName]§! has revealed the cause of the meteorological heat spikes.\n\nGeothermal vents and geysers across the surface are ejecting highly flammable gases which then accumulate in the atmosphere. Electrostatic discharges then act as an ignition catalyst, causing explosive weather fronts that ravage the skies of this world.\n\nThese reactions have given our [Root.Owner.GetEngineerPlural] new insights into thermodynamic combustion, with applications towards our own fleet's propulsion systems.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:316`

## extreme_frontiers.420.desc.traumatized

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.420.desc.traumatized&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，这项危险的研究让[leader.GetName]受到严重的精神创伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

Unfortunately, the hazardous nature of this research has left [leader.GetName] with severe traumatization.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:317`

## extreme_frontiers.420.desc.maimed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.420.desc.maimed&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，这项危险的研究让[leader.GetName]受到严重的身体创伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Unfortunately, the hazardous nature of this research has left [leader.GetName] with severe physical injuries.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:318`

## CHTHONIAN_PRESERVATION_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACHTHONIAN_PRESERVATION_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

冥界生物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

Chthonian Biomass

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:321`

## chthonian_preservation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achthonian_preservation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.GetName]§!的扫描显示这是一颗死亡行星，它曾经是气态巨行星，但大气层被§Y[from.System.Star.GetName]§!强烈的辐射剥离了。\n\n尽管如此，它的地表上还是探测到了非常痕量的生物质，需要进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Surveys of §Y[from.GetName]§! reveal it to be a chthonian planet, a former gas giant stripped of its atmosphere by the intense radiation of §Y[from.System.Star.GetName]§!.\n\nDespite this, very faint traces of biomass have been detected on its surface, warranting further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:322`

## extreme_frontiers.430.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.430.title&lang=en)

### 中文

冥界保存

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Chthonian Preservation

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:323`

## extreme_frontiers.430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!上的生物质信号竟然是来自一小群缇扬奇。\n\n它们可能是在这颗气态巨行星的大气消散前的一个临界点到来的，随后被熔融金属包裹着凝固了。\n\n虽然有少量的生物物质留存，但在长期暴露在炼狱级的恒星辐射下，它们的大部身体已经变成了一种独特的生物金属合金。\n\n§Y[leader.GetName]§!相信，对这些珍惜的化石进行更细致的研究我们可以发现一种以生物原材料生产合金的新方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

The biomass readings on §Y[from.GetName]§! are the petrified remains of a pod of Tiyanki.\n\nLikely present in the gas giant during a critical point in its atmospheric dissipation, their bodies were encased in molten metal that has since solidified.\n\nWhile a small amount of biological matter remains inside, prolonged exposure to the chthonian-level stellar radiation has converted the majority of their corpses into a unique bio-metallic alloy.\n\n§Y[leader.GetName]§! believes that with closer study of these rare fossils, we will be able to discover new avenues of alloy production from biological sources.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:324`

## CHTHONIAN_PETRIFICATION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACHTHONIAN_PETRIFICATION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

冥界化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Chthonian Petrification

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:325`

## CHTHONIAN_PETRIFICATION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACHTHONIAN_PETRIFICATION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural]们通过研究§Y[from.GetName]§!上缇扬奇兽群的化石遗骸，旨在完全理解炼狱级核辐射对生物质的影响，并有可能发现全新的高级合金生产方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

By examining the petrified remains of the fossilized Tiyanki pod on §Y[from.GetName]§!, our [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] aim to fully understand the effects of chthonian-level radiation on biological matter, potentially opening up new means of advanced alloy production.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:326`

## extreme_frontiers.435.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.435.title&lang=en)

### 中文

生物制合金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Bio-Produced Alloy

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:327`

## extreme_frontiers.435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对§Y[from.From.GetName]§!上化石化的缇扬奇遗骸进行细致的调查后，我们研究出了一种模拟这种长时间炼狱级辐射暴露效应的新的合金产出方式。\n\n这一突破性发现带来了一种杰出的制造方式，它使用生物质作为主要原料，而产出却可以与传统的方法相媲美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Following careful examinations of the petrified Tiyanki remains on §Y[from.From.GetName]§!, we have successfully identified a new means of alloy production by simulating the effects of long-term chthonian-level radiation exposure.\n\nThis breakthrough offers a remarkable methodology which uses biological matter as its primary raw material, producing results that rival more conventional methods.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:328`

## tech_alloy_fossilization

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_alloy_fossilization&lang=en)

### 中文

合金化石化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Alloy Fossilization

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:329`

## tech_alloy_fossilization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_alloy_fossilization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将生物质暴露在模拟的炼狱级辐射中，我们就可以将细胞结构石化为可用的合金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

By exposing biological material to simulated chthonian-level radiation, we can fossilize cellular structures into usable metallic alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:330`

### English

§HUnlocks building§!: ['building:building_bio_furnace']

## building_bio_furnace

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_bio_furnace&lang=en)

生物熔炉

### English

Bio-Furnace

## building_bio_furnace_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_bio_furnace_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将大量模拟恒星辐射结合起来，该设施能够将生物质转化为合金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

By incorporating significant levels of simulated stellar radiation, this facility is capable of converting biological matter into alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:333`

## ORBITAL_RESONANCE_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AORBITAL_RESONANCE_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

黄金比例

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Perfect Ratio

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:336`

## orbital_resonance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorbital_resonance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.System.GetName]§!内的公转周期展现出了引人注目的规律。每颗环绕恒星的行星都以两倍其相邻行星的周期公转着，精确到千分之一秒。\n\n这种怪异的精确比值值得调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Orbital durations in the §Y[from.System.GetName]§! system exhibit a remarkable pattern. Each planet orbits the star in exactly double the time of its neighbor, accurate to over a thousandth of a second.\n\nThis anomalously perfect ratio warrants investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:337`

## extreme_frontiers.440.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.440.title&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道共振

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

Orbital Resonance

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:338`

## extreme_frontiers.440.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.440.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.System.GetName]§!是我们目前为止所遇见的解释轨道共振的最佳案例。\n\n每一颗行星在和邻居到达最近点时都相当于一个周期性引力源，产生了弹弓效应和动量互锁。这种相互作用的不同寻常的稳定性产生了这种高度规律的轨道。\n\n该恒星系为研究行星引力对途径物体运动的加速提供了极好的机会，并可能应用于舰船机动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

§Y[from.System.GetName]§! exhibits the most perfect example of orbital resonance we have ever encountered.\n\nEach planet exerts a periodic gravitational force on its neighbor when they are at their nearest points, creating a catapult effect and mutually dependent momentum. The unusual stability of these interactions results in highly coordinated orbits.\n\nThe system offers an excellent opportunity to study the way planetary gravity accelerates the motion of passing objects, with possible applications to ship maneuvering.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:339`

## GRAVITY_ASSIST_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGRAVITY_ASSIST_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

重力辅助研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Gravity Assist Studies

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:340`

## GRAVITY_ASSIST_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGRAVITY_ASSIST_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

研究§Y[from.GetName]§!精准的轨道共振效应将会提升我们对舰船机动中行星引力弹弓效应的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

Studying the perfect orbital resonance effect on §Y[from.GetName]§! will improve our understanding of planetary slingshot maneuvers on ships.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:341`

## extreme_frontiers.445.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.445.title&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道弹弓进展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

Orbital Slingshot Advancements

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:342`

## extreme_frontiers.445.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.445.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究了§Y[from.From.System.GetName]§!恒星系内独特的轨道共振效应之后，我们对规划舰船最佳飞行路线的方法有了巨大的进展。\n\n这些新的模型可以极其充分地在舰船用光速加减速阶段利用行星引力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

After studying the unique orbital resonance effect in the §Y[from.From.System.GetName]§! system, we have greatly improved our methods for plotting optimal ship flight paths.\n\nThese new models will allow us to take greater advantage of planetary gravity in achieving ship acceleration and deceleration at sublight speeds.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:343`

## tech_orbital_maneuvers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_orbital_maneuvers&lang=en)

### 中文

先进轨道机动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Advanced Orbital Maneuvers

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:344`

## tech_orbital_maneuvers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_orbital_maneuvers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对行星重力井最大化利用的先进计算可以用于进一步优化亚光速旅行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Sublight travel can be further optimized by performing advanced calculations that make maximum use of planetary gravity wells.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:345`

## site_absolute_zero

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_absolute_zero&lang=en)

### 中文

绝对零度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Absolute Zero

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:352`

## site_absolute_zero_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_absolute_zero_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的地质扫描显示在一个特定的地下区域中，局部的极端温度接近绝对零度。\n\n对内部更详尽的研究将会带来更多的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Topographical scans of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! show extreme temperatures approaching absolute zero localized in a particular subterranean region.\n\nA more detailed study of the interior should yield more answers.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:353`

## extreme_frontiers.1000.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1000.title&lang=en)

### 中文

冰封

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Frozen Blockage

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:354`

## extreme_frontiers.1000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在寻找§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的极端低温源时，§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了一座远古地下设施的入口。\n\n堵住入口的是一层厚厚的岩石和冰塔，似乎将整座设施封冻了上千年。\n\n入口处干冰的升华从封堵处的许多裂隙升腾出了柱柱白烟。我们的设备显示其中有一座巨大的复合体；[leader.GetName]确信极端冷源就在里面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

While searching for the source of the extremely low temperatures on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, §Y[leader.GetName]§! has discovered the entrance to an ancient underground structure.\n\nBlocking the entrance is a mass of frozen rock and ice seracs that have likely rendered the structure itself inaccessible for millennia.\n\nMelting carbon dioxide in the entrance is emitting plumes of gas from various gaps in the blockage. Our equipment indicates a vast complex within; [leader.GetName] is confident the source of the extreme cold can be found inside.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:355`

## extreme_frontiers.1000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

凿穿封堵处

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Break through the blockage.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:356`

## extreme_frontiers.1005.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1005.title&lang=en)

### 中文

时光冻结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

Frozen in Time

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:357`

## extreme_frontiers.1005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!成功突破了堵在§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上远古设施入口处的冰层。\n\n在这个空间里有几十具某种未知爬行物种的冻尸。他们扭曲的身体靠着墙被冻结在原地，墙上的爪痕表明他们绝望地试图逃跑。\n\n从入口渗出的干冰烟雾的一定来自地下更深的地方，有一座螺旋形阶梯通向复合体的更深处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! has successfully breached the ice blocking the entrance of the ancient structure on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nInside the space are dozens of frozen corpses belonging to an unknown reptilian race. Their contorted bodies are frozen in place against the walls, with claw marks indicating a desperate attempt to escape.\n\nThe source of the dry ice plumes found seeping through the entrance must be deeper underground, with a spiral stairwell leading further into the complex.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:358`

## extreme_frontiers.1010.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1010.title&lang=en)

### 中文

冰封墓穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Glacial Tomb

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:359`

## extreme_frontiers.1010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在冰封的设施深处，§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了原始但理论上可运作的冷冻舱填满了整个中央舱室。\n\n这里表现出的极端严寒远超这些设施所设计的温度范围。这些舱室内的居住者早已死了数千年。\n\n少数没有被冰雪封冻的舱室底部的基柱上还能看得清一些字，写的是里面的人的社会头衔和等级。等级越高的的舱室越靠近一个深埋在冰霜中的巨大中央荚舱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

Deeper in the frozen complex, §Y[leader.GetName]§! has uncovered a large central chamber filled with primitive but theoretically functional cryogenic chambers.\n\nThe extreme cold present here far exceeds the designed temperature ranges of these devices. The occupants of the pods have died thousands of years ago.\n\nWhat few chambers have not been reclaimed by ice and snow have readable plinths at the base, denoting the social title and rank of those contained within. The higher ranked chambers are positioned closest to a large central pod, which is itself deeply embedded in frost and snow.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:360`

## extreme_frontiers.1010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调查中央舱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

Investigate the central pod.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:361`

## extreme_frontiers.1015.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1015.title&lang=en)

### 中文

冰封君王

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

Frozen Monarch

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:362`

## extreme_frontiers.1015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!破开了§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上冰封墓穴中央的大舱。\n\n在其中我们发现的是一个远古王室首领的安息之地，这一点在之前的舱室中发现的文本有提及。和他一起冻眠的还有几十个随从，而他自己的冷冻舱隆重地装饰着外墙。\n\n这位死去君王的高科技石棺通过迷宫般的管道和电路与一台古老的机器相连。这太装置被嵌在一大块冰中，它的温度低到危险的程度，连分析设备都无法承受。\n\n这台设备的故障的原因以及它与行星冰冻核心的关系仍有待进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! has breached the large central pod at the heart of the glacial tomb on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nInside we discovered the final resting place of the head of the ancient royal house hinted at in the texts of the previous chamber. Frozen alongside are dozens of their retainers whose own cryopods adorn the walls in ceremonial fashion.\n\nThe dead ruler's technological sarcophagus is connected via a labyrinthine network of tubes and circuitry to an ancient machine. This device is embedded into a large body of ice, the temperature of which approaches dangerously low levels to even our analysis equipment.\n\nWhat caused the apparent malfunction of this facility and its relationship to the planet's frozen core requires more study.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:363`

## extreme_frontiers.1015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这里出了什么事？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

What went wrong here?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:364`

## extreme_frontiers.1020.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1020.title&lang=en)

### 中文

低温灾难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Cryogenic Catastrophe

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:365`

## extreme_frontiers.1020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上冰封墓穴中央舱室的分析解释了这场灾难发生的缘由。\n\n冷冻机械装置是使用亚热动力学，利用了行星极低的核心温度。在君王感染了绝症之后，人们希望能在治疗方法问世时唤醒王室的随从。\n\n尽管理论像那么回事，但当独特设计的循环介质泄漏到行星核心时，吸热反应失控了。随之而来的致命降温迅速冻结了整座设施。\n\n我们采纳了他们最初的理论，并加以改进，在专门用于冰封星球的先进低温技术方面取得了突破。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Analysis of the glacial tomb's central chamber on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! has revealed the origin of the disaster that unfolded.\n\nThe cryogenic machinery makes use of subthermal dynamics which take advantage of this planet's extremely low core temperatures. After the monarch was infected with a terminal disease, the hope was to reawaken the royal retinue when a cure was discovered.\n\nWhile their theory was sound, a runaway endothermic reaction occurred when their uniquely engineered circulation medium leaked into the planetary core. This triggered a fatal temperature drop which quickly flash-froze the entire facility.\n\nWe have taken their initial theory and improved upon it, achieving a breakthrough in advanced cryogenic technology specialized for use on frozen worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:366`

## tech_cryovault

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryovault&lang=en)

### 中文

冰封星球栖息地

### English

Frozen World Habitats

## tech_cryovault_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_cryovault_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用在冰封星球上发现的亚热动力学，我们可以在更寒冷的气候中创造更宜居的生活条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

By harnessing the subthermal dynamics found in the environments of frozen worlds, we can create more hospitable living conditions in colder climates.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:368`

## building_cryovault_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_cryovault_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座利用冰冻气候的亚热动力学来创造可控而舒适生活空间的封闭栖息地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

A sealed habitat that utilizes the subthermal dynamics of frozen climates to create a controlled and comfortable living space.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:371`

## site_toxic_matrix

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_toxic_matrix&lang=en)

毒液池

### English

Toxic Pools

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:374`

## site_toxic_matrix_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_toxic_matrix_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的表面几乎完全被一些整齐排列的圆形小池塘覆盖。这种规整的网格表明它们可能是精心设计的而非自然形成。现场调查可能带来更多的解答。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!'s surface is almost entirely covered by a lattice grid of small, circular, liquid-filled pools. Their neat alignment suggests deliberate engineering rather than natural formation. A field expedition may provide more answers.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:375`

## extreme_frontiers.1100.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1100.title&lang=en)

### 中文

破裂粘液囊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Ruptured Slime Sac

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:376`

## extreme_frontiers.1100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的大部分地表变成了一列列精心排列的球形空洞。这些圆形的小盆地中填满了黏稠的毒液。\n\n在其中一个池子里发现了一个破裂的有机囊体，表面覆盖着凝胶状的黏液。囊的外膜似乎是被从内部刺破的。\n\n至于这个囊是否就是池中液体的来源仍有待研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Most of the surface of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! has been worked into a series of spherical hollows arranged in carefully aligned rows. These small rounded basins are filled with a viscous toxic fluid.\n\nDiscovered within one of the pools was a ruptured organic sac coated in a gelatinous mucus. The sac's membrane appears to have been pierced from the inside.\n\nWhether or not this sac is the source of the liquid inside the pools remains to be discovered.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:377`

## extreme_frontiers.1100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1100.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

Continue searching.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:378`

## extreme_frontiers.1105.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1105.title&lang=en)

### 中文

毒孕育池

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

Toxic Gestation Pools

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:379`

## extreme_frontiers.1105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的黏液池进行分析发现了异常的放射性生长激素，这通常与有机孕育周期有关。\n\n囊的内层似乎是一层复杂的肉质膜，能够调节黏液渗透的速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Analysis of the slime pools on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! identified unusually radioactive growth hormones commonly associated with organic gestation cycles.\n\nThe lining of the sac itself appears to be a sophisticated fleshy membrane, capable of regulating the rate at which the slime permeates through.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:380`

## extreme_frontiers.1105.desc.non_toxoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1105.desc.non_toxoid&lang=en)

### 中文

我们认为发现的是某种物种高度适应毒性环境的繁育颊囊。进一步探索可以带来更多的解答。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

We believe we have discovered the leftover gestation pods of a species with a particular adaptation to toxic environments. Further exploration should provide more answers.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:381`

## extreme_frontiers.1105.desc.toxoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1105.desc.toxoid&lang=en)

### 中文

这些颊囊和我们自己的繁育周期有这令人震惊的相似性，或许是某种我们的远亲物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

These pods bear a striking similarity to our own reproductive cycles and are perhaps some form of distantly related species.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:382`

## extreme_frontiers.1105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1105.a&lang=en)

继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Continue investigating.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:383`

## extreme_frontiers.1110.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1110.title&lang=en)

### 中文

胚胎残余

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Embryonic Remains

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:384`

## extreme_frontiers.1110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的黏液池中发现的一个破裂的繁育囊含有一个几乎发育完全的生命形式。\n\n显微分析表明，这个胚胎标本中存在复杂的神经系统，其神经特性通常意味着智慧生命。\n\n最后一组黏液池还有待检验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

One of the ruptured gestation sacs found in the slime pools of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! contained the remains of a nearly fully-developed lifeform.\n\nMicroscopic analysis of this embryonic specimen suggests the presence of a complex nervous system with neural qualities commonly associated with intelligent life.\n\nOne final array of pools remains to be examined.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:385`

## extreme_frontiers.1110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

检测最后一组池子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Examine the last of the pools.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:386`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.title&lang=en)

### 中文

发现生命信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Life Signs Detected

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:387`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

在检测§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上剩下的黏液池时，§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了一具完整的繁育囊，其中还探测到了生命信号。\n\n然而在提取的过程中，颊囊的肉质薄膜发生了破裂，这具标本死去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

While examining the remaining slime pools on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, §Y[leader.GetName]§! found a fully-intact gestational sac with life signs detected within.\n\nHowever, during the extraction process the sac's fleshy membrane ruptured and the specimen perished.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:388`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.desc.non_toxoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.desc.non_toxoid&lang=en)

### 中文

里面的小尸体呈现出不同寻常的生物特征，包括充满放射性血浆的血管和分泌精神活性化合物的内腺体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

The small cadaver inside exhibited unusual biological features, including veins filled with radioactive plasma and internal glands that secrete psychoactive compounds.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:389`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

研究确定，包括胚胎在内的整个繁育囊都是通过向高能的有毒粘液中插入一种特殊的化学催化剂产生的。\n\n我们认为找到了一个未知物种的废弃古繁殖地。这是它们生殖周期的正常组成还是人工设施，我们可能永远不得而知。通过在原位培养，我们也许能够再繁育出一个活体样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

It has been determined that the entire gestational sac, including the embryonic creature within, was spawned within the toxic slime by inserting a specialized chemical catalyst into the energy-dense slime.\n\nWe believe we have found the abandoned ancient breeding grounds of an unknown species.Whether this was a normal part of their reproductive cycle or an artificial facility, we may never know. By incubating a sample in situ, we may be able to grow another living specimen.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:390`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.desc.toxoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.desc.toxoid&lang=en)

### 中文

亦或者我们可以小剂量地将这些丰富而有效繁育黏液作为营养补充剂在[leader.Owner.GetName]各处发放。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

Alternatively, we could distribute small doses of this rich and potent gestational slime as a nutritional supplement across the [leader.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:391`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

催化黏液。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Catalyze the slime.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:392`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.b&lang=en)

### 中文

从黏液中提取能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Extract the energy from this slime.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:393`

## extreme_frontiers.1115.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1115.c&lang=en)

### 中文

分发营养补充剂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Distribute nutritional supplements.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:394`

## TOXIC_SLIME_CATALYZATION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATOXIC_SLIME_CATALYZATION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

毒性黏液催化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Toxic Slime Catalyzation

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:395`

## TOXIC_SLIME_CATALYZATION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATOXIC_SLIME_CATALYZATION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过催化一些从[from.Planet.GetName]取回的毒性繁育黏液，我们或许可以从中孵出生物体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

By catalyzing some of the toxic gestational slime recovered from [from.Planet.GetName] we may be able to spawn a living organism from it.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:396`

## extreme_frontiers.1120.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1120.title&lang=en)

### 中文

孵化成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Spawning Successful

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:397`

## extreme_frontiers.1120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过催化一份从§Y[from.From.Planet.GetName]§!取回的毒性繁育黏液样品，我们得以诱导数十个含有胚胎的繁育囊孵化。\n\n内部的生命体快速发育成熟后用特化的颚从内部刺穿了半透明的繁育囊膜。\n\n它们看起来非常地温顺而乐于合作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

By catalyzing a sample of the gestational slime recovered from §Y[from.From.Planet.GetName]§!, we were able to induce the spawning of dozens of gestational sacs, each harboring an embryo.\n\nWhen the rapidly developing organisms reached maturity, they pierced the translucent membranes of their gestational sacs from within using specially adapted mandibles. Slowly they emerged from the thick mucus, groggy and disoriented.\n\nThey appear to be docile and fully cooperative.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:398`

## extreme_frontiers.1120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetName]我们会给它们一席之地的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

[Root.Owner.GetName] will find a purpose for them.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:399`

## extreme_frontiers.1120.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1120.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Owner.GetName]的[Root.Owner.GetResearcherPlural_lower]们会好好利用它们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

[Root.Owner.GetName]'s [Root.Owner.GetResearcherPlural_lower] will make use of them.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:400`

## trait_slimeborn_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_slimeborn_desc&lang=en)

该物种在繁育催化剂的辅助下从浓稠的黏液中快速孵化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

This species spawns rapidly from a thick and mucilaginous slime with the aid of a gestational catalyst.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:404`

## tech_living_test_subjects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_living_test_subjects&lang=en)

### 中文

活体实验对象

### English

Living Test Subjects
