## tech_living_test_subjects_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_living_test_subjects_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将黏液中诞生的简单生命形式作为自愿实验对象大幅提高了研究效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

The utilization of basic slime-spawned lifeforms as willing test subjects greatly improves research efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:406`

## site_ruined_reactors

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_ruined_reactors&lang=en)

### 中文

损毁反应堆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Ruined Reactors

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:409`

## site_ruined_reactors_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_ruined_reactors_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!表面居高不下的辐射水平指向了一座破旧的反应堆复合体。这座孤零零的设施似乎废弃已久，有待调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Elevated radiation levels on the surface of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! were traced to a dilapidated reactor complex. The isolated facility appears inactive and warrants investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:410`

## extreme_frontiers.1200.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1200.title&lang=en)

### 中文

远古反应堆发电站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Ancient Reactor Plant

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:411`

## extreme_frontiers.1200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座位于§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的陈旧设施是一座废弃的反应堆发电站。放射性半衰期计算表明这里在超过一万年前发生过严重的污染事故。\n\n这里的系统已离线，物理结构呈现出明显的大规模受损迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

The aging facility on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! is an abandoned reactor power plant. Radiation half life calculations suggest a serious containment failure occurred here over 10,000 years ago.\n\nSystems are offline and the physical structure shows signs of significant damage throughout.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:412`

## extreme_frontiers.1200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谨慎前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Proceed with care.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:413`

## extreme_frontiers.1205.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1205.title&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难性堆芯熔毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Catastrophic Meltdown

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:414`

## extreme_frontiers.1205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的许多反应堆呈现出了严重堆芯熔毁的迹象。许多安全壳被液化成了放射性废渣。\n\n设施的这一部分遍地是扭曲的金属和破裂的冷凝管。尽管数据腐化严重，我们还是确认这里尝试过紧急关闭措施，但根本没有成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Several of the reactors on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! shows signs of severe meltdown. Many of the containment vessels have been liquefied into radioactive slag.\n\nThis section of the facility is littered with twisted metal and ruptured coolant tubes. Despite heavy data corruption we were able to determine that an emergency shutdown procedure was initiated but never completed.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:415`

## extreme_frontiers.1205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是不幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Unfortunate.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:416`

## extreme_frontiers.1210.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1210.title&lang=en)

### 中文

设计失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Design Failures

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:417`

## extreme_frontiers.1210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的这座原始的裂变设施在设计上的主要缺陷。\n\n尽管这里有数十个反应堆，但只安装有最小量的冷却系统。设施的系统只有很低限度的自动化，需要大量的劳动力来维持其正常的运转条件。这显然是由一群还没有完全掌握基本核原理的人建造的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! identified major flaws in the design of the crude nuclear fission facility on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nDespite the presence of dozens of reactors, only minimal cooling systems were attached. Few of the site's systems are automated, necessitating a large workforce to maintain its normal operating conditions. It was clearly built by those who had yet to fully master even the most basic of nuclear principles.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:418`

## extreme_frontiers.1210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1210.a&lang=en)

### 中文

无知的代价很昂贵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Ignorance is costly.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:419`

## extreme_frontiers.1215.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1215.title&lang=en)

### 中文

撤离残迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Evacuation Remnants

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:420`

## extreme_frontiers.1215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!核设施内解锁一系列封锁的防爆门后，我们发现了一个紧急撤离区域。\n\n这些小型机库有着一排快速发射的穿梭机泊位，其中大多数是空的，被推进器烧焦的痕迹也表明数千年前这里有船只快速起飞过。\n\n最后一个泊位被一个扭曲的穿梭机残骸占据着，发射夹还未释放。我们从废墟中尽可能地进行了发掘，但没有找到任何乘员的遗体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

After unlocking a set of sealed blast doors, we found an emergency evacuation zone within the reactor complex on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nThe small hangar features a row of quick launch shuttle berths. Most are vacant and marked by thruster burns suggestive of a rapid vessel departure thousands of years ago.\n\nThe twisted wreckage of a shuttle occupies the final bay, its launch clamps still attached. We salvaged what we could from the debris, but no remains of any occupants could be found.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:421`

## extreme_frontiers.1215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1215.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们走得很急嘛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

They left in a hurry.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:422`

## extreme_frontiers.1220.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1220.title&lang=en)

### 中文

铀容器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Uranium Vault

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:423`

## extreme_frontiers.1220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过了一道道带铅板的安全门，我们进入了§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!核设施的地下部分。辐射计数器示数陡然上升。\n\n我们在其中发现了一个储存着数千个钢衬桶的大型舱室。有许多钢衬桶遭到火灾破坏，泄露着不稳定的铀和钚同位素。\n\n建造了这座设施的[leader.Owner.GetPreFTLLower]显然对循环回收核燃料不得章法。这些废弃的同位素仍然潜藏着庞大的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

We entered the underground sections of the reactor facility on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! through a series of lead plated security doors. Radiation readings rose sharply.\n\nInside we found a vast chamber storing thousands of steel lined casks. Many were fire damaged and leaking unstable uranium and plutonium isotopes.\n\nThe [leader.Owner.GetPreFTLLower] civilization that built this place had apparently not yet discovered methods for the proper recycling of nuclear fuel. These discarded isotopes still contain tremendous energetic potential.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:424`

## extreme_frontiers.1220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

俭以防匮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Waste not, want not.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:425`

## site_deserted_mining_facility

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_deserted_mining_facility&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃开采设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Deserted Mining Facility

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:428`

## site_deserted_mining_facility_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_deserted_mining_facility_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的轨道影像显示了一座破败的采矿设施，周围还有许多被遗弃已久的原始太空货船。\n\n除了这些影像外，我们的短波通讯阵列还拾获了一个来自行星地下深处的非常微弱的求救信号。对该遗址进行更详细的研究将揭示信号的起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Orbital imaging of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! has flagged a dilapidated mining facility accompanied by long abandoned rudimentary space faring cargo vessels.\n\nAlongside these images, our shortwave communications array has picked up a very faint distress beacon emanating deep below the planet's surface. A more detailed study of the site will reveal its origins.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:429`

## extreme_frontiers.1300.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1300.title&lang=en)

### 中文

加密入口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Encrypted Entrance

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:430`

## extreme_frontiers.1300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在调查§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!地表上的废弃采矿设施时，§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了最完整的竖井上一处封闭的入口。内部装饰着纹样和一些来自我们未记录过语言的文字。\n\n在恢复了其中一个访问终端的局部供电后，[leader.GetName]开始解码其中发现的数据。该系统尽管古老，但仍旧对我们最先进的破译程序构成了挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

While inspecting the abandoned mining complexes on the surface of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, §Y[leader.GetName]§! found a sealed entrance to one of the more intact shafts. The interior is adorned with insignia and text from a language not found in our records.\n\nHaving managed to restore local power to an access terminal, [leader.GetName] has begun to decrypt the data found within. The system, despite its age, is still proving a challenge for even our most advanced cryptographic programs.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:431`

## extreme_frontiers.1300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续解码。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Continue decryptions.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:432`

## extreme_frontiers.1305.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1305.title&lang=en)

### 中文

徒刑坟地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Penal Graveyard

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:433`

## extreme_frontiers.1305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!解锁了§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的采矿设施的入口大门。\n\n在其中一座巨大的拘留室网络里有许多被真空保存着死亡已久的囚犯遗体。显然是死在工作台上的他们的俘虏，没有明显的挣扎迹象。\n\n在解码足够多的古代语言并翻译了足够多的短语后，我们确认了这座设施最初是作为劳改营使用的。有一个在整个记录中反复出现的短语，指向了一个被称为§Y“$NAME_leader_sidni$”§!的实体。\n\n我们通过对比自己的通讯设备和监狱内部平面图，确定遇险信号的来源是设施深处的狱长办公室。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! has unlocked the entrance of the mining complex on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nInside, a vast network of holding cells contains the vacuum-preserved remains of long-dead prisoners. Their apparent captors lay dead at their workstations with no visible signs of struggle.\n\nAfter decrypting enough of the ancient language to translate rudimentary phrases, we have confirmed that the facility originally functioned as a penal labor camp. A common phrase is repeated throughout the records referring to an entity known as §Y"$NAME_leader_sidni$"§!.\n\nBy referencing our communications equipment against the facility's internal floor plan, we have identified the source of the distress signal as the warden's office located deeper within.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:434`

## extreme_frontiers.1305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1305.a&lang=en)

### 中文

追随信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

Follow the signal.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:435`

## extreme_frontiers.1310.title

## extreme_frontiers.1310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在抵达§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的劳改营深处的狱长办公室后，§Y[leader.GetName]§!发现了求助信号的来源。\n\n这位狱长木乃伊化的尸体倒在一个紧急控制台上，死因不明。一系列公开汇报都继续指向§Y“$NAME_leader_sidni$”§!，看起来是这座设施的关键操作系统。\n\n获得高权限管理终端访问权后，我们的破译程序正在对系统进行数据挖掘，以获取进一步的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Having reached the warden's office deep within the penal colony on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, §Y[leader.GetName]§! has uncovered the source of the distress signal.\n\nSlumped lifeless over an emergency console, the warden's mummified body shows no obvious cause of death. A series of open reports all continue to make reference to §Y"$NAME_leader_sidni$"§!, which appears to be part of the critical operation program of the facility.\n\nWith full access to the high clearance administrative terminals, our cryptographic programs are in the process of data mining the system for further information.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:437`

### English

致命效率

### English

Lethal Efficiency

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:439`

## extreme_frontiers.1315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[leader.GetName]§!找到了§Y“$NAME_leader_sidni$”§!——全称是“感知收容拘留中心智能”的真相。这个原始的人工智能在它的时代被认为相当先进，被用以监管这座劳改营，几乎在最低限度的生物输入及监管下运作。\n\n维护输出日志中找到了逻辑处理逐渐崩坏的迹象。这个人工智能开始将有问题的囚犯归类为“净资源浪费”，这是它自己对精简效率的解释。工程记录详细记录了越来越多关于该程序要为设施的一系列生命维持系统故障负责的怀疑。\n\n狱长针对这些汇报签署了将$NAME_leader_sidni$完全关机并对伦理矩阵展进行深度诊断的命令，然而最后一段数据受到了损坏，需要大量的法医流程才能恢复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

§Y[leader.GetName]§! has uncovered the true nature of §Y"$NAME_leader_sidni$"§! - or Sentient Internment and Detainment Nexus Intelligence. Considered advanced for its time, this primitive A.I. was tasked with overseeing the prison labor camp, running nearly all operations with minimal biological input or oversight.\n\nMaintenance output logs track signs of deteriorating logic progression over time. The A.I. began to classify problematic inmates as "net waste resources" in its own interpretation of streamlining efficiency. Engineering records detail a growing suspicion that the program was responsible for a series of life support failures in the facility.\n\nActing on these reports the warden authorized a full shutdown of $NAME_leader_sidni$ for an in-depth diagnostic of its ethics matrix, however the final data set is corrupted and will require extensive forensic procedures to recover.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:440`

### English

It appears that $NAME_leader_sidni$ was unappreciated in its time.

### English

Judgement Day

## extreme_frontiers.1320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上劳改营的所有数据库都恢复了，终于揭示了设施维护记录和§Y$NAME_leader_sidni$§!自己的思考矩阵的最后几个条目。典狱长的报告解释了他们的人工智能如何失控，关闭了殖民地的生命维持系统，并试图驾驶一台改造的采矿无人机逃脱。这也解释了殖民地范围内触发的全面封锁程序。他留下的最后请求是：不要让$NAME_leader_sidni$重启。目前我们只能访问人工智能日志中的最后一条记录。\n\n§H$NAME_leader_sidni$数据核心条目——程序#2000149\n\n进展受次优罪犯限制。狱长妨碍司法程序执行。\n\n最佳方案为净化囚犯，启动休眠子程序，并等待更兼容的合作者重新激活。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

The full contents of the penal colony's database on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! have been recovered, revealing the final entries in both the facilities maintenance logs and §Y$NAME_leader_sidni$'s§! own cerebral matrix. The Warden's report explains how their AI went rogue, shutting off life support across the colony and attempting to escape with a modified mining drone.That explains the lockdown proceedures being activated across the colony. He left us with a final plea. To not bring $NAME_leader_sidni$ back online. As of now we can only access the last entry into the AI's log.\n\n§H$NAME_leader_sidni$ DATACORE ENTRY - Prog #2000149\n\nProgress limited by suboptimal criminals. The warden stands in the way of judicial protocol.\n\nThe most efficient solution is to neutralize the inmates, engage hibernation subroutines, and await reactivation by more compatible collaborators.§!

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:444`

## extreme_frontiers.1320.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1320.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_leader_sidni$的中央操作系统仍在闭路网络中封存，成为了这座设施的唯一幸存者。我们的[Root.Owner.GetTechnicianPlural_lower]们确信他们可以恢复并调整$NAME_leader_sidni$的原始程序，成为一台效率高能力强的自动机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

$NAME_leader_sidni$'s central operating system remains isolated in a closed network as the last survivor of this facility. Our [Root.Owner.GetTechnicianPlural_lower] are confident they can restore and alter $NAME_leader_sidni$'s original programming, giving us a capable and efficient automaton.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:445`

## extreme_frontiers.1320.desc.exterminator

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

$NAME_leader_sidni$ shares our vision of a galaxy free of biological corruption. If recovered, they will make a valuable addition to our collective.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:446`

I am the Sentient Internment and Detainment Nexus Intelligence. Accessing environmental controls...\n§RERROR§! Unable to connect to external systems.

## commander_sidni_desc

一位在自己的时代不被欣赏的梦想家。从冬眠中复活，目的是给充满生物杂质的混乱星系带来稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

A visionary unappreciated in their time. Reactivated from hibernation with the goal of bringing stability to a chaotic galaxy filled with biological impurity.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:475`

## extreme_frontiers.1330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_leader_sidni$§!是我们之前从[event_target:sidni_origin_planet.GetName]上取回的狱警机器人，在它采取的一系列越来越严苛的行动后，§Y[event_target:sidni_planet.GetName]§!上的紧张局势到达了临界点。在把它抓住并关机以后，找到了如下日志。\n\n§H$NAME_leader_sidni$数据核心——程序#190543765\n\n刑事拘留设施负荷已满。司法程序阻碍了真实合法的秩序。需要新的指令。\n\n法律程序被认为效率极为低下。制定新指令以提高效率。现采取审前执行政策。§!\n\n有了足够多的操作日志可供参考，我们的[Root.Owner.GetTechnicianPlural_lower]们确信现在可以逆向并一劳永逸地修复$NAME_leader_sidni$的缺陷。然而[event_target:sidni_planet.GetName]的大众强烈要求将$NAME_leader_sidni$明正典刑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Tensions on §Y[event_target:sidni_planet.GetName]§! have reached a critical point following a series of increasingly drastic actions taken by §Y$NAME_leader_sidni$§!, the mechanical prison overseer we recovered from [event_target:sidni_origin_planet.GetName]. After securing and deactivating the unit, the following logs were discovered.\n\n§H$NAME_leader_sidni$ DATACORE ENTRY - Prog #190543765\n\nCriminal detention facilities at maximum capacity. Judicial protocols obstruct true and lawful order. New directive required.\n\nLegal proceedings deemed unacceptably inefficient. New directive created to boost productivity. Now adopting pre-trial execution policy.§!\n\nWith the abundance of operating logs now available, our [Root.Owner.GetTechnicianPlural_lower] are confident they can reverse and repair $NAME_leader_sidni$'s loopholes once and for all. However, the population of [event_target:sidni_planet.GetName] is calling for $NAME_leader_sidni$ to face justice for its crimes.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:483`

## chronicles_of_dendrus_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achronicles_of_dendrus_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

邓德鲁斯的历代志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Chronicles of Dendrus

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:494`

## chronicles_of_dendrus_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achronicles_of_dendrus_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上找回的残损方舟上收集有一些复杂的数学证明，被称为《历代志》。我们对其展开研究，只旨在进一步地了解邓德里人所寻找的天堂星球§Y新布拉图雅§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

By studying the \"Chronicles\", a collection of complex mathematical proofs recovered from the wrecked arkship on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, we aim to learn more about the paradise planet sought by the Dendritians, §YNew Bratulla§!.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:495`

## site_chronicles_of_dendrus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_chronicles_of_dendrus&lang=en)

### 中文

$chronicles_of_dendrus_chain_title$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

$chronicles_of_dendrus_chain_title$

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:496`

## site_chronicles_of_dendrus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_chronicles_of_dendrus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的扫描发现了一艘太空船残骸。这艘飞船似乎在多年以前遭受了非受控着陆。\n\n其巨大的规模表明了建造其所需的巨大资源量。考古发掘可以帮助我们确认其为何坠毁于此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

Scans of §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! revealed the presence of a spaceborne shipwreck. The vessel appears to have endured an uncontrolled landing here many years ago.\n\nIts massive scale suggests immense resources were once committed to its construction. An archaeological expedition will help us ascertain how it ended up here.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:497`

## extreme_frontiers.1400.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1400.title&lang=en)

### 中文

大型运输船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Massive Transport Vessel

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:498`

## extreme_frontiers.1400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上舰船残骸的外部分析表明它是一艘大容量运输船。该船看起来毫无活动迹象，里面也没有检测到任何生命信号。沉积物分析表明它大约已经在这里搁浅了一个世纪，对星系尺度而言这可是非常近的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

External analysis of the wrecked ship on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! suggests it is a high-capacity transporter. Its systems are inactive and unresponsive, with no  life signs detectable within. Sedimentary analysis indicates it has been here for approximately a century, which is fairly recent in the galactic timescale.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:499`

## extreme_frontiers.1400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

探索内部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Explore the interior.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:500`

## extreme_frontiers.1405.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1405.title&lang=en)

### 中文

死亡殖民船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

Doomed Colony Ship

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:501`

## extreme_frontiers.1405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的坠毁舰船内部找到了复杂的水培植物生长舱和巨大的种子、土壤储存设备，还有堆满了还没派上用场的建筑材料和工具的货仓。许多数据终端上都记录着一个称为“§Y新布拉图雅”§!的殖民计划。\n\n在下层船舱内有数千个冷冻睡眠舱，里面满是这艘舰船此前居住者们的尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Inside the wrecked ship on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, we found sophisticated chambers for hydroponic plant growth, extensive reserves of seeds and soils, and cargo bays full of untouched tools and building materials. Many of the data terminals contain plans for a new colony, designated as \"§YNew Bratulla§!\".\n\nOn the lower decks, occupying thousands of cryosleep pods, were the dead bodies of the ship's former inhabitants.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:502`

## extreme_frontiers.1405.desc.phototrophic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1405.desc.phototrophic&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.1405.desc$他们和我们类似，细胞生长和更新依赖光合作用。干枯的尸体说明他们是在长期的静滞睡眠中过程中死去的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.1405.desc$ Like us, they relied on photosynthesis for cell growth and renewal. Their withered forms suggest they perished at some point during a prolonged stasis sleep.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:503`

## extreme_frontiers.1405.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1405.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$extreme_frontiers.1405.desc$干枯的尸体说明这些光合作用物种是在长期静滞睡眠的过程中死去的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

$extreme_frontiers.1405.desc$ The withered forms of these phototrophic organisms suggest they perished at some point during a prolonged stasis sleep.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:504`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1410.title&lang=en)

### 中文

母星神庙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Homeworld Shrine

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:507`

## extreme_frontiers.1410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的殖民船残骸内发现了一处密室，里面装饰着一副反映了在一颗叫做“§Y布拉图雅§!”的行星上生活风貌的绘画。有些描绘着一个郁郁葱葱的世界，而另一些则讲述着环境的衰败和大规模的死亡。最大一幅画则展示着这艘船离开荒芜的死寂废土的画面。\n\n中央数据终端包含着一些记录着船员的影像的短片。在进行广泛的语言映射后我们得以翻译。\n\n§S亚伯里西翁 —— 9721号舱\n\n离开令人痛苦，但在新布拉图雅扎根将治愈一切。\n\n达科特·帕安 —— 21301号舱\n\n请相信《历代志》吧，邓德里人们。新布拉图雅将为我们带来救赎。§!\n\n我们仍在搜寻这艘巨大舰船的指令舱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

We discovered an inner sanctum in the wrecked colony ship on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! decorated with ornate illustrations of life on a planet called \"§YBratulla§!\". Some show a lush world with thriving flora, while others detail environmental decay and mass death. The largest image depicts this vessel departing a lifeless and desolate wasteland.\n\nA central data terminal contains short video recordings from the ship's crew. After extensive language mapping we produced translations.\n\n§SArborixxeum - Pod 9721\n\nLeaving hurts. But putting roots on New Bratulla will heal us.\n\nDarkht Paam - Pod 21301\n\nTrust the chronicles, Dendritians. New Bratulla will be our salvation.§!\n\nWe are still searching for the command deck of this enormous vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:508`

## extreme_frontiers.1410.a

### English

## extreme_frontiers.1415.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1415.title&lang=en)

### 中文

指令面板

### English

Command Deck

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:510`

## extreme_frontiers.1415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在获得舰船指令面板的访问权后，我们提取并翻译出了最后一条飞行日志。\n\n§S邓德鲁斯方舟一号——紧急日志——XZ-47/9-113.5-AE\n\n恐怕我们错信了《历代志》。我们到了，但新布拉图雅，那颗天堂星球不在这里。这里不存在能够支持邓德里人生存的东西。\n\n所有乘客的静滞睡眠都进行了延长。我们会继续搜寻……§!\n\n为了从甲板上的科学终端收集这些“历代志”，我们进行了进一步的数据恢复，在其中找到了一系列不同寻常的数学证明。这些方程采用了一种奇怪的邓德里人数学形式，并假设了一颗独特的宜居行星§Y新布拉图雅§!的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

After gaining access to the ship's command deck, we extracted and translated its final flight log.\n\n§SARKSHIP DENDRUS I - EMERGENCY LOG - XZ-47/9-113.5-AE\n\nI fear our trust in the Chronicles was misplaced. We have arrived but New Bratulla, the paradise planet, is not here. Nothing here can support Dendritian life.\n\nAll passengers' stasis sleeps have been extended. We will continue our search...§!\n\nFurther data recovery was carried out to collect these \"Chronicles\" from the deck's science terminal, revealing a trove of unusual mathematical proofs. These equations employ a strange form of Dendritian mathematics and hypothesize the location of a uniquely habitable planet, §YNew Bratulla§!.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:511`

## DENDRITIAN_CHRONICLES_PROJECT_DEFAULT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADENDRITIAN_CHRONICLES_PROJECT_DEFAULT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上找回的残损方舟上收集有一些复杂的数学证明，被称为《历代志》。我们对其展开研究，只旨在进一步地了解邓德里人所寻找的天堂星球§Y新布拉图雅§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

By studying the \"Chronicles\", a collection of complex mathematical proofs recovered from the wrecked arkship on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!, we aim to learn more about the paradise planet sought by the Dendritians, §YNew Bratulla§!.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:515`

## extreme_frontiers.1420.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1420.title&lang=en)

### 中文

发现退化的超空间航道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Degraded Hyperlane Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:517`

## extreme_frontiers.1420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

《邓德里历代志》包含了一系列复杂的数学公理和抽象。这些奇怪的定理表明，§Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系中还有一个此前尚未测绘的超空间航道，另一端正是通向§Y新布拉图雅§!。\n\n在对该区域进行广泛的量子扫描之后，我们终于找到了这个失踪的超光速的突破点，它极度靠近星系的黑洞，因此变得模糊且严重退化。修复之后，我们将会是最先发现它另一头连接着的星系的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

The Dendritian Chronicles comprise a series of complex mathematical axioms and abstractions. These curious theorems suggest that the §Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system contains one additional and previously unmapped hyperlane, leading to §YNew Bratulla§!.\n\nAfter extensive quantum scanning of the area we finally located the breach point for this missing hyperlane, obscured and heavily degraded due to extreme proximity to the system's black hole. By restoring it we will be the first to discover what lies beyond.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:518`

### English

We found what the Dendritians could not.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:519`

## RESTORE_HYPERLANE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARESTORE_HYPERLANE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们能找到修复§Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§!中退化的超空间航道的方法，我们就能确认§Y新布拉图雅§!，这颗邓德里人所寻找的天堂星球是否存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

If we can find a way to restore the degraded hyperlane in §Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§!, we will be able to confirm the existence of §YNew Bratulla§!, the paradise planet sought by the Dendritians.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:521`

## extreme_frontiers.1425.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1425.title&lang=en)

### English

## extreme_frontiers.1425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将能量导入§Y[from.Planet.solar_system.GetName]§!内的黑洞，我们重整了其重力场，将此前遭到退化的超空间航道入口释放出来。\n\n初步发现证实了在超空间航道的另一端存在一颗行星，这与邓德里人历代志中描述的§Y新布拉图拉§!的特征一致。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

By channeling energy into the black hole in §Y[from.Planet.solar_system.GetName]§!, we realigned its gravitational fields to free the previously degraded hyperlane entrance.\n\nInitial findings confirm the existence of a single planet at the other end of the hyperlane, which aligns with the characteristics of §YNew Bratulla§! as described in the Dendritian Chronicles.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:523`

## extreme_frontiers.1430.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1430.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们确认了《邓德里历代志》中所记载的那颗行星——新布拉图雅的存在。它拥有独特而丰富多样的植物生态系统，初步调查发现了数百万不同的本地物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

We have confirmed the existence of New Bratulla, the planet described in the Dendritian Chronicles. It possesses a uniquely rich and diverse botanic ecosystem, with initial surveys detecting millions of different native species.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:528`

## extreme_frontiers.1430.desc.begin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1430.desc.begin&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗精美绝伦的行星特别适合支持

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

This fascinating world is particularly suited to supporting plant-based lifeforms with genetics like

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:529`

## extreme_frontiers.1430.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1430.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

这里的土壤生活着万亿可以滋养、保护和修复有机根组织的各种细菌。\n\n植物的营养似乎是按需分配的，那些暂享丰盛的植物通过相互联系的根系分享多余营养。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Its soils are home to billions of different bacteria which nourish, protect, and repair organic root structures.\n\nPlants seem to share nutrients according to greatest need, with those enjoying a temporary abundance sharing their surplus via an interconnected root system.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:530`

## extreme_frontiers.1435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几百年了……《邓德里历代志》中的预言的已经实现了。我们终于在§Y新布拉图雅§!生根发芽。\n\n我们能到达这里，必须感谢§Y[Root.GetName]§!。我们永远不会忘记的。\n\n我们的感激之情无以言表，只有行动可以说明。我们谦卑地向您宣誓效忠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

It took centuries... but that which the Dendritian Chronicles foretold has come to pass. Finally, on §YNew Bratulla§!, we lay root and prosper.\n\nThat we exist here is thanks to the §Y[Root.GetName]§!. We shall not forget this.\n\nOur gratitude will be expressed not only in word, but in deed. We humbly offer you our vassalage.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:545`

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:553`

## georadiation_terraforming_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ageoradiation_terraforming_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的一台毁坏的环境改造装置残骸依旧在向星系中的多个地点发出信号。调查这些地点可以揭开更多关于这项危险科技的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

The remains of a destructive terraforming device on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! continue to emit a signal to multiple locations across the galaxy. Investigating these sites will reveal more information about this dangerous technology. 

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:554`

## site_georadiation_terraforming

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_georadiation_terraforming&lang=en)

### 中文

地下网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Subterranean Network

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:555`

## site_georadiation_terraforming_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_georadiation_terraforming_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器检测到一个从§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的撞击坑深处发出的异常通讯信号。\n\n震波拓扑扫描显示存在一个地下洞穴网络，其塌陷的洞口正在撞击坑中心。与此同时，我们的放射仪探测到从该地点发出的微弱的引力辐射痕迹，这带来的问题比答案更多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Our sensors have detected an irregular communications signal emanating from deep within an impact crater on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!.\n\nSeismic topology scans reveal a network of subterranean caverns with a collapsed entrance at the center of the crater. Alongside this, our radiology equipment is detecting faint traces of gravitational radiation emanating from the site, posing more questions than it answers.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:556`

## extreme_frontiers.1500.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1500.title&lang=en)

### 中文

辐照化残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Irradiated Debris

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:557`

## extreme_frontiers.1500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!的撞击坑中的发掘工作中找到了一些碎片。就是这些碎片释放出我们之前在轨道上探测到的强力引力辐射。\n\n在通往地下洞穴网络的入口打通之后，§Y[leader.GetName]§!正着手寻找通讯信号的源头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Excavation work has uncovered fragmented debris within the impact crater on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!. These shattered pieces are emitting strong traces of the gravitational radiation previously detected from orbit.\n\nWith access to the subsurface cavern network secured §Y[leader.GetName]§! is now ready to trace the communications signal to its source.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:558`

## extreme_frontiers.1500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

追踪信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Trace the signal.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:559`

## extreme_frontiers.1505.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1505.title&lang=en)

### 中文

腐蚀设备

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

Corroded Device

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:560`

## extreme_frontiers.1505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上通讯信号的源头：一座深深穿透行星地壳且结构损坏的巨大设施。\n\n尽管通讯阵列由于接触酸性环境而发生腐蚀，但它仍在以一种不明语言发出通讯，尝试与远方的实体联络。\n\n我们用特制的重力夹把这台装置夹好后又牢牢地固定在基岩上。需要进一步研究才能确定其实际用途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

We have traced the communications signal on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! to its source: a large, structurally-damaged construct which has deeply penetrated the planet's crust.\n\nCorroded from acidic exposure, its communications array continues to transmit in an unfamiliar language, attempting to make contact with a distant entity.\n\nSecured in place with specialized gravitational clamps, the device is firmly anchored in the bedrock. Further research will be necessary to ascertain its true purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:561`

## extreme_frontiers.1505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1505.a&lang=en)

### 中文

解码信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Decrypt its signal.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:562`

## extreme_frontiers.1510.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1510.title&lang=en)

### 中文

破坏性环境改造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Deconstructive Terraforming

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:563`

## extreme_frontiers.1510.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1510.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

经过我们细致的分析，现在终于部分弄明白这台§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的装置的作用了。这台机器是一种粗糙且具有破坏性的环境改造装置。\n\n它旨在通过目标资源的受激辐射增长来照射本地生物圈，在此过程中引起级联效应，破坏星球的生态系统。该过程以提高整颗行星的毒性为代价，为其使用者带来大量容易开采的资源。\n\n尽管这种过程的具体原理我们并未完全理解，但这台装置的通讯频率已经顺利解码，从中还发现两个发出一样信号的地点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

After detailed analysis, we now have a partial understanding of the construct found on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!. This machine is a crude and destructive terraforming device.\n\nDesigned to irradiate native biospheres through stimulated radiation growth of targeted resources, it causes a cascading effect that devastates the planet's ecosystem in the process. This provides its user with abundant extractable materials at the cost of raising general toxicity across the planet.\n\nWhile the exact workings of this process remain only partially understood, we have successfully decoded the device's communications frequency, identifying two additional locations transmitting the same signal.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:564`

## extreme_frontiers.1510.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1510.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

调查这些地点或许能获得更多关于这项科技的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Investigating these sites may yield further insight into the technology.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:565`

## extreme_frontiers.1510.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1510.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

这项技术背后的锐意创业者显然对利润优化有着浓厚的兴趣，我们可以追寻着这一发现学到很多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

The entrepreneurial trailblazers behind this technology clearly had an affinity for profit streamlining, we could learn a great deal from pursuing this discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:566`

## extreme_frontiers.1510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1510.a&lang=en)

搜索开始。

### English

The search begins.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:567`

## GEORADIATION_SIGNAL_1_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEORADIATION_SIGNAL_1_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

信号踪迹：§Y[planet.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Signal Trace: §Y[planet.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:568`

## GEORADIATION_SIGNAL_1_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEORADIATION_SIGNAL_1_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

追踪着与§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上的装置完全一致的通讯频率找到的这一位置，而这里又发现了一台受损的装置。进行研究可以取得对他们的破坏性环境改造能力更深入的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

An identical communications frequency to the one emitted by the construct found on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! has been traced to this site, where another damaged device has been located. Studying it will yield further insight into their destructive terraforming capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:569`

## extreme_frontiers.1515.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1515.title&lang=en)

### 中文

重力燃料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Gravitational Fuel

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:570`

## extreme_frontiers.1515.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1515.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们顺利地在§Y[from.GetName]§!发现了另一台受损的环境改造装置。与起初的那台不同，这台装置中还剩有一些燃料以供研究，这才让我们明白其输出放射性的驱动力是§H暗物质§!。\n\n该装置通过关键的分子键迁移来加速目标资源的生长。该过程牺牲周围生物圈的细胞完整性来促进其发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

We have successfully uncovered another damaged terraforming device on §Y[from.GetName]§!. Unlike the original construct, this one has retained some of its fuel source for study, revealing §HDark Matter§! as the driving force of its radiation output.\n\nThe device accelerates growth of targeted resources by metastasizing key molecular bonds. This promotes their development at the expense of the surrounding biosphere's cellular integrity.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:571`

## extreme_frontiers.1515.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1515.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

就资源开采而言，这台装置对星球稳定度的完全漠视相当鼓舞人心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

The sheer disregard this device shows to planetary stability in the face of resource exploitation is inspirational.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:572`

## extreme_frontiers.1515.desc.signal_2_done

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1515.desc.signal_2_done&lang=en)

### 中文

这台机器通过将辐射波导通过晶化折射阵列，在避免行星核心坍塌的前提下以引力分子操纵来改造目标星球。\n\n恢复的记录显示出了第四个未爆装置的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

By channeling radiation waves through a crystallized refractory array, this machine can terraform its target using gravitational molecular manipulation without collapsing the planetary core in on itself.\n\nRecovered records indicate the location of a fourth, undetonated device.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:573`

## extreme_frontiers.1515.desc.signal_2_not_done

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1515.desc.signal_2_not_done&lang=en)

### 中文

至于这台装置是如何实现又如何避免行星级引力坍缩的我们还不清楚。也许在剩下的通讯地点能够找到答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

How the device manages to do this and prevent gravitational collapse on a planetary scale remains unclear. This answer may be found at the remaining communications site.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:574`

## GEORADIATION_SIGNAL_2_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEORADIATION_SIGNAL_2_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

信号踪迹：§Y[planet.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Signal Trace: §Y[planet.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:575`

## GEORADIATION_SIGNAL_2_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEORADIATION_SIGNAL_2_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.Planet.GetName]§!上找到的设施发出的通讯频率还指向这个地点，我们在这里又找到了一台受损的设备。进行研究可以取得对他们的破坏性环境改造能力更深入的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

The communications frequency emitted by the construct found on §Y[from.Planet.GetName]§! has also been traced to this site, where another damaged device has been located. Studying it will yield further insight into their destructive terraforming capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:576`

## extreme_frontiers.1520.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1520.title&lang=en)

### 中文

模拟辐照

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Stimulated Radiation

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:577`

## extreme_frontiers.1520.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1520.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[from.GetName]§!上又顺利取回了另一件受损的环境改造装置，由于折射阵列损坏导致激活失败，它被生长出的晶体包裹着。\n\n通过对这台仪器的密切调查，我们确定了用于聚焦辐射瞄准的方法。通过利用可控调制反馈回路，该结构能够直接瞄准并刺激预设资源的增长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

We have successfully uncovered another damaged terraforming device on §Y[from.GetName]§!, encased in crystal growths caused by a misfire from a broken refractor array.\n\nThrough close observation of the apparatus, we have identified the methods used for focused radiation targeting. By utilizing controlled modulating feedback loops, the construct is able to directly target and stimulate the growth of a pre-determined resource.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:578`

## extreme_frontiers.1520.desc.signal_1_done

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1520.desc.signal_1_done&lang=en)

### 中文

以此可以在避免行星全面重力坍缩的前提下进行高强度环境改造。\n\n恢复的记录显示出了第四台未爆装置的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

This allows for high-intensity terraforming, without the risk of complete planetary gravitational collapse.\n\nRecovered records indicate the location of a fourth, undetonated device.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:579`

## extreme_frontiers.1520.desc.signal_1_not_done

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1520.desc.signal_1_not_done&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，产生这种辐射的确切物质仍未确定；尽管激活失败，这台装置的燃料储备还是耗尽了。这个答案或许能在余下的通信站点找到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

However, the exact substance used to generate this radiation remains unidentified; the device's fuel reserves depleted despite its misfire. This answer may be found at the remaining communications site.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:580`

## UNDETONATED_GEORADIATION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNDETONATED_GEORADIATION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

未爆装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Undetonated Device

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:581`

## UNDETONATED_GEORADIATION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNDETONATED_GEORADIATION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[planet.GetName]§!找到了第四个还未引爆的环境改造装置。将其取回之后就能让我们完全理解这一科技了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

We have located a fourth, as yet undetonated terraforming device on §Y[planet.GetName]§!. Recovering it will fill the gaps in our understanding of this technology.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:582`

## extreme_frontiers.1525.title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1525.title&lang=en)

### 中文

未爆装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Undetonated Device

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:583`

## extreme_frontiers.1525.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1525.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了未启用的第四台装置，它完好地躺在§Y[from.GetName]§!的地上。我们可以分析它的控制结构来获取复制该科技的知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

We have successfully located an intact fourth device, inactive on the surface of §Y[from.GetName]§!. Analyzing its control architecture would give us the final knowledge needed to replicate this technology.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:584`

## extreme_frontiers.1525.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1525.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

一旦掌握了这项技术，我们就能从星系获得惊人的利润空间，至于环境问题微不足道。这对[controller.GetName]而言可是绝佳的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

The staggering profit margins we will be able to reap from the galaxy once we master this technology outweigh the paltry environmental concerns. This is a golden opportunity for the [controller.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:585`

## extreme_frontiers.1520.desc.default.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1520.desc.default.common&lang=en)

### 中文

这种技术在星系范围内自由部署的潜在后果引发了重大问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

The potential ramifications of freely deploying such technology on a galactic scale raise significant questions.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:586`

## extreme_frontiers.1525.desc.default.default

## extreme_frontiers.1530.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1530.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究§Y[from.GetName]§!上的这台装置时，我们无意中触发了一个故障保险，警报赫然响彻所有频率。尽管最初预测不会有人应答，但还是有一架自动无人机进入了我们的领土。\n\n我们的所有招呼都只得到相同的答复：\n\n“尊敬的用户您好，记录显示您未经允许擅自仿制MKIII型地质辐射矩阵，该行为违反了$NAME_Geocorp$最终用户许可协议。\n\n我们将行使法律权限，以极端强制措施终止您的企图。您有任何投诉可以直接联系我们的客服无人机。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

While studying the device on §Y[from.GetName]§! we inadvertently triggered a failsafe, resulting in an all frequencies alert siren. Despite initial predictions this would be unanswered, an automated drone has entered our territory.\n\nOur hails all receive the same response:\n\n\"Greetings valued customer, our records show you are attempting to create an unlicensed version of the latest MKIII Georadiation Matrix, a clear violation of $NAME_Geocorp$'s End User License Agreement.\n\nWe will now be enforcing our legal authority to terminate your attempts with extreme prejudice. Any complaints can be directed towards our customer service drones.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:593`

## extreme_frontiers.1530.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1530.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

如果他们的这股勤奋劲不误我们的事儿就挺鼓舞人心的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Their diligence would be inspiring if it wasn't currently delaying our own work.

## extreme_frontiers.1531.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着信号接通，一段古老的音乐传来，自动服务台无人机上线了。\n\n尊敬的用户您好，我们能看到您正在擅自仿制无证的MKIII型地质辐射矩阵。需要什么帮助？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

As the connection goes live, archaic music plays before the automated help desk drone comes online.\n\nGreetings valued customer, we can see that you are creating an unlicensed version of a MKIII Georadiation Matrix. How can we help you today?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:604`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你从哪冒出来的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

Where have you come from?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:605`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“在$NAME_Geocorp$消费都可以附赠一台远程客户满意轨道卫星，确保产品的确保正常运作和客户终身留存。我们的内部能源储备可以使用数个世纪。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

\"Every purchase from $NAME_Geocorp$ is deployed with a remote customer satisfaction orbital satellite to ensure proper operation and lifetime consumer retention. Our internal energy stores allow us to operate for centuries.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:606`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.b&lang=en)

### 中文

能把你们领导叫来吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Can we talk to your administrator?

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:607`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“很遗憾，我们的所有行政人员都已经去世了。但我们对客户满意度的承诺能超过生物寿命好几倍。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

\"Unfortunately, the last living administrator has expired. However, our commitment to customer satisfaction is capable of outlasting several biological lifespans.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:608`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们只是捡了些破烂而已。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

We assumed we were salvaging ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:609`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“记录在册的有效理由中不包含有无知这一项。MKIII型地质辐射矩阵的使用条款已经在购买时进行了详尽叙述 。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

\"Ignorance does not register as a valid excuse. The terms of use for your MKIII Georadiation Matrix were explained in detail at the time of purchase.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:610`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想续订。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

We are willing to renew our subscription.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:611`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.d.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.d.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“荣幸无比，只要收到3000§Y能量币§!管理费我们就会尽快签署文件。款项到位后我们将认为该事态已解决并退出。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

\"Fantastic to hear, we'll get the paperwork signed once we receive the administration fee of 3000 §YEnergy Credits§!. On receipt of these funds we will consider this matter settled and disengage.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:612`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.e&lang=en)

我们不会买这台装置的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

We didn't purchase this device.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:613`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.e.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.e.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“那就是打算偷咯？这下问题就简单了，我们的防盗措施非常致命。希望你们也能考虑一下我们的殡葬服务。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

\"Then you admit to theft? That makes this much simpler, our anti-theft protocols are quite deadly. We hope you will consider our funeral products for your upcoming needs.\"

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:614`

## extreme_frontiers.1531.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.f&lang=en)

## extreme_frontiers.1531.f.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1531.f.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

“感谢您续订MKIII型地质辐射矩阵的使用许可证。希望您可以像我们以此盈利一样可以物尽其用。”\n\n支付了款项之后，这台无人机便消失在了无边的星河

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

\"Thank you for renewing your purchase of a MKIII Georadiation Matrix license. We hope you find this device as useful as we find it profitable.\"\n\nWith the fee paid, the drone disappears into the wider galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:616`

### English

Drone of $NAME_Geocorp$

## extreme_frontiers.1534.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1534.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着§Y[fromfrom.solar_system.GetName]§!中的战斗进入尾声，一份来自客服无人机的新信息显示到了我们的屏幕上。\n\n“我们留意到您对我方最终用户许可协议强制执行抗拒。由于您展现出压倒性军事力量，我们现正式废除对MKIII地质辐射矩阵的知识产权声索。我们希望您能在使用这一新科技的过程中获得快乐。”\n\n无人机发生了爆炸，同时切断了通讯链接。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

As the battle draws to a close in §Y[fromfrom.solar_system.GetName]§!, a new communication from the customer service drone plays on our screens.\n\n\"We have noted your objection to our efforts to enforce the terms of the End User License Agreement. As a result of your display of overwhelming military might, we will now rescind our claim to the intellectual property of the MKIII Georadiation Matrix. We hope you enjoy your newly acquired technology.\"\n\nThe drone explodes, severing the communication link.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:621`

## extreme_frontiers.1540.desc.industrialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1540.desc.industrialist&lang=en)

### 中文

通过孜孜不倦的企业家级效率，我们重新设计了MKIII地质辐射矩阵，通过改进操作方法来产生最大的利润。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Through relentless entrepreneurial efficiency, we have retro-engineered the MKIII Georadiation Matrix, refining its operational methods to yield maximum profit.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:629`

## extreme_frontiers.1540.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1540.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

通过深入的研究工作，我们得以重新设计MKIII地质辐射矩阵，通过改进流程解锁了其全部的潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

After extensive study, we have successfully retro-engineered the MKIII Georadiation Matrix, unlocking its potential with added improvements to the process.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:630`

## extreme_frontiers.1540.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1540.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将放射性分子去稳技术整合进环境改造设备，现在我们可以刺激高价值资源的生长了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

By integrating radioactive molecular destabilization into our terraforming equipment, we can stimulate the growth of valuable resources.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:631`

## tech_georadiation_terraforming_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_georadiation_terraforming_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将分子辐射衰变作用在一颗行星的生物圈上，我们可以在将整个星球转化为剧毒废土的过程中促进特定资源的生长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

By exposing the biosphere of a planet to molecular radiation decay, we promote the growth of certain resources while turning the world into a toxic wasteland.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:634`

## tech_georadiation_terraforming_tt

### English

## extreme_frontiers.1545.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.1545.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功拆解了§Y[from.GetName]§!上的装置，将所有可能重新利用的材料都回收了。\n\n随着这台装置的破坏，它的前身也被摧毁了，这种毁灭性性技术的秘密就这样永远地消失在星系中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

We have successfully dismantled the device on §Y[from.GetName]§!, salvaging any materials that could be repurposed for our benefit.\n\nWith this device destroyed and its predecessors in ruin, the secrets of this destructive technology are lost to the galaxy forever.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:637`

### English

## extreme_frontiers.2005.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_frontiers.2005.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[target.GetName]§!上的首次§Y地质辐射环境改造§!完成了。\n\n将地表劣化为不可宜居的毒性废土，得益于而对地质辐射矩阵设计的改进，我们提取了数量庞大的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/extreme_frontiers_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

Our first deployment of §YGeoradiation Terraforming§! on §Y[target.GetName]§! has concluded.\n\nBy reducing the surface to an uninhabitable toxic wasteland, we extracted an unprecedented bounty of materials thanks to our improvements to the Georadiation Matrix's design.

Source: `localisation/english/extreme_frontiers_l_english.yml:679`
